# Feature & Product Decisions
> Requires: CLAUDE.md

Resolved product, feature scope, business rules, compliance, and UX decisions. Load this when building a specific feature to understand what was decided and why.

For architecture and stack decisions see `plan/docs/DECISIONS-ARCH.md`.

## Active & Recent Decisions (#108 – #206)

| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| 108 | **Booking payment methods are a fully dynamic, admin-managed list** (Settings → Payment Methods). Structured `accountName` + `accountNumber` fields and `isEnabled` toggle replace legacy `accountInfo`. QR images stored at `assets/payment-methods/{method}/{filename}` in Firebase Storage. |
| 109 | **Protected payment methods:** `pay-at-hotel` and `add-to-bill` cannot be deleted from Settings or via direct API calls (`PROTECTED_PAYMENT_METHODS`). |
| 110 | **In-room store payment methods unified:** `settings/hotelConfig.paymentMethods[]` is the single source for booking, store, and corporate payment visibility. |
| 111 | **Independent surface visibility switches:** each payment method owns `isEnabled` (booking), `showInStore` (store), and `showInCorporate` (corporate). |
| 112 | **Google Sign-In is authentication only;** Spark Rewards enrollment requires explicit consent via Privacy Policy / Terms checkbox. |
| 113 | **Homepage featured room cards do not expose live per-room operational status.** Cards are type-driven marketing cards; operational status is not rendered on homepage cards. |
| 114 | **Self-service cross-provider auth linking is deferred to Phase 2.** Provider conflict message directs guests to sign in with their existing method first. |
| 115 | **BIR/VAT scope decision:** tax breakdown (12% VAT, VATable Sales, VAT-Exempt Sales, VAT Amount) calculated client-side for reports & XLSX exports. |
| 116 | **Direct-paid store orders join the shared collections ledger on delivery.** COD recorded as Cash; Add to Bill remains unsettled booking-folio charge. |
| 117 | **Early departure retains the contracted booking total.** Operational checkout shortens dates without auto-reducing total price. |
| 118 | **Spark Rewards points require a fully settled folio** and cover net booking spend only. |
| 119 | **Production environment split:** production database starts clean for bookings and transactions; active staff accounts pre-provisioned. |
| 120 | **Notification Center (Phase 12):** persisted `notifications` collection for admin alerts with 30-day retention cron. |
| 121 | **Purpose of stay is required at physical check-in and defaults to `Leisure`.** Add to admin drawer & guest registration form. |
| 122 | **Confirm with balance (CWB-01..05):** allows staff to transition a `payment-uploaded` booking to `confirmed` with an intentional partial payment. Reuses `unpaidCheckoutApprovalThreshold` (default 5,000) as four-eyes gate. |
| 123 | **Multi-booking picker for `/my-booking`:** when email-alone search matches >1 booking, returns a privacy-preserving list of 10 most recent stays. |
| 124 | **Unify payment reference number on payment ledger:** retired top-level `paymentReferenceNumber` in favor of per-payment `transactionReference`. |
| 125 | **HEIC client-side conversion:** use `heic-to@1.5.2` (LGPL-3.0) via dynamic `import()` for HEIC photo conversions in admin guest ID upload. |
| 126 | **Multi-booking picker privacy tightening:** uniform row shape with `maskedEmail` (`j***@gmail.com`), omitting `guestName`. |
| 127 | **Walk-in first/last name split:** modal collects first and last name separately, dropping string split. |
| 128 | **Drop `guestName` from email-alone 1-match single card:** public `/my-booking` does not reflect guest name back for email-alone queries. |
| 129 | **Turnstile token reuse for picker clicks:** prevents "Bot verification token missing" error during multi-booking navigation. |
| 130 | **Search form stays always-mounted:** suppresses with `hidden` / `aria-hidden` rather than unmounting, keeping Turnstile widget alive. |
| 131 | **Masked email on single-booking lookup card:** public `/my-booking` never exposes full email or guest name on any lookup path. |
| 132 | **Breakfast served map hydration:** `AdminContext` snapshot mapper hydates `breakfastServed` to persist "Mark Served" toggles across refreshes. |
| 137 | **Editable Terms & Conditions & consent versioning:** terms body editable from Settings; booking consent captures version string. |
| 138 | **Guest store search & category browsing:** intercom store panel includes live search bar and category filters. |
| 139 | **House Rules in transactional emails:** embedded in payment-confirmed, booking-confirmed, and check-in reminder templates. |
| 141 | **Walk-in Payment Method from Settings (WPM):** walk-in modal payment selector dynamically loads from `settings/hotelConfig.paymentMethods[]`. |
| 142 | **New Booking & Customizable Booking Sources (NBS):** renamed modal to "New Booking", widened source to string, admin-editable booking sources in Settings. |
| 144 | **Child Capacity & Occupancy Rules (CHD):** max 2 children, adult and child capacity validated independently, extra beds apply to adult or child overflow. |
| 145 | **Extra Bed Pricing & Capacity (EXB):** per-room-type `maxExtraBeds` and `extraBedRate`, snapshotted on booking, extra bed total included in folio. |
| 146 | **Discount Scope Configuration (DSC):** admin-toggleable scope (room / breakfast / extra bed) per discount class (Senior/PWD, Voucher, Member). |
| 147 | **Pending Booking Expiry & Hold Window (PEX):** 24h default payment hold window, `holdExpiresAt` timestamp snapshot, daily cleanup cron (`/api/holds/expire`). |
| 148 | **DSC Scope Snapshots & Senior Guardrails:** `discountScopeSnapshot` persisted on booking; senior/PWD scope restricted to admin with statutory warning. |
| 149 | **VAT Breakdown Calculation (DSC-06):** 12% VAT, VATable Sales, VAT-Exempt Sales (Senior/PWD), and VAT Amount calculated client-side for reports & XLSX exports. |
| 150 | **VAT Breakdown Surfaces (DSC-07):** receipt PDF, admin drawer, and guest confirmation preview display 12% VAT reconciliation. |
| 151 | **Weekend Rate Visibility (WRV):** Step 1 room cards and Step 3 breakdown display non-regular nightly rate lines whenever non-regular rates apply. |
| 159 | **Multi-Room Bookings & Reservation Header (MRB):** reservation header collection `reservations/{id}` with `reservationRef` (`R-YYYYMMDD-NNNNN`), linking child bookings. |
| 164 | **Reservation Header Idempotency & Write Operations:** transactional pre-allocated `reservationId` UUIDv4 + `requestFingerprint` SHA-256 for create, walk-in, reschedule, and corporate flows. |
| 165 | **Structured, snapshotted cancellation policy (CRL-05):** cancellation policy fields (`cancellationCutoffHours`, `cancellationRefundPctBefore`, `cancellationRefundPctAfter`) added as runtime-editable numbers in Legal Content settings. Reservations snapshot the policy fields and source at creation time. Evaluator handles cutoff rules independent of DST/local offsets, with legacy null snapshots falling back to standard 48-hour/100% rules and `"legacy-fallback"` source. |
| 166 | **MRB-13 Cancellation Scope Model (proposed 2026-08-02):** `POST /api/bookings/cancel` gains an optional `scope: "room" \| "reservation"` (default `"room"` for byte-compatible single-child behavior). When `scope === "reservation"` and the booking has a `reservationId`, one transaction cancels every cancellable child, decrements voucher/corporate code `usageCount` exactly once per shared code (not per child), runs the MRB-05 loyalty clawback per-child, and updates the reservation header (`cancelledRoomCount` + `activeRoomCount` + `paymentStatus: "cancelled"` when the resulting `activeRoomCount === 0`). The first-created child has no special financial consequence because no child owns the reservation folio (`reservations/{id}/payments` and `refunds/` are the source of truth). Admin BookingsPage cancel modal shows a `This room` / `All N rooms` scope selector when `reservationRoomCount > 1`; guest `/my-booking` cancel modal always cancels the whole reservation and the copy states the room count. One reservation-scope `booking-cancelled` email lists every cancelled room + aggregate state (MRB-09 expands the template inventory). Auth is unchanged: the existing `ref + (email \| token)` guest credential is sufficient because the server resolves `reservationId` from the child doc. Gates: CRL-06 (guest policy preview), CRL-07 (refund liability + admin workflow), MRB-09 (full reservation-scope email template inventory), MRB-10 (guest lookup by reservation). Rejected: a new `/api/reservations/cancel` sibling (duplicates the auth + audit path); reservation-owned cancel that bypasses per-child CRL-02 audit metadata (silently drops Reports rows). |
| 167 | **Corporate multi-room negotiated rate + N-room usage cap (MRB-08, proposed 2026-08-02):** `/api/bookings/create` resolves the negotiated rate **per stay type** from `corporateCodes/{code}.ratePerRoomType` (then falls back to the room type's flat `corporateRate`, then the standard `pricePerNight`) instead of once for the primary type. The "Continue without code" flat-rate path applies the same per-stay fallback chain. The corporate code's `usageCount` increments by `assignedRooms.length` (N rooms = N uses), and the cap check inside the create transaction rejects when `usageCount + N > usageCap`. On `handleCancelBooking`'s full-reservation path, the same `usageCount` is decremented by the cancelled room count (the MRB-13 scope selector controls the count: `scope === "room"` decrements 1, `scope === "reservation"` decrements `activeRoomCount`). Rejected: keeping the single-rate model (silently overcharges/undercharges a mixed-type corporate block); `usageCount += 1` regardless of room count (turns a capped code into effectively unlimited at N>1); introducing a per-room `corporateUsageCount` (the cap is contract-level, not per-physical-room). The `/corporate/book` page mirrors `BookingPage`'s room cart from MRB-06: per-stay type + own adults/children/extra beds, claimed rooms filtered out, and submission blocked until every stay is creatable. Negotiated pricing + LOU wording, the "no public promo voucher" rule (decision #100), and the "Continue without code" intent flag (BI-04) all carry over unchanged. Gates: MRB-09 (one email per reservation), MRB-10 (guest lookup nests children), MRB-11 (Reports use reservation owners). |
| 168 | **Reservation-scope email fan-out (MRB-09, proposed 2026-08-02):** every guest-facing booking email (`booking-submitted`, `payment-confirmed`, `booking-confirmed`, `checkin-reminder`, `booking-confirmed-with-balance`, `booking-rescheduled`, plus the receipt PDF) renders a single "block" view that lists every room in the reservation, not just the first child's data + a flat rate breakdown line. The view is built by `buildReservationEmailView(reservation, children)` (exported from `guest-app/server/handlers/email.ts`) which synthesises a synthetic `rooms[]` array of per-stay projections (position + ref + type + occupancy + breakfast + per-stay total) and an aggregate total. The `bookingRows` helper + receipt PDF + subject lines detect the `rooms[]` array and switch into the multi-room shape; N=1 falls through to the legacy single-room render (byte-equivalent to pre-MRB-09). The new `booking-cancelled-reservation` action + template fires from MRB-13's reservation-scope cancel path; the per-child `booking-cancelled` action stays for the per-child path. The checkin reminder cron groups confirmed bookings by `reservationId` and sends one email per reservation (N duplicate reminders for an N-room reservation were the worst pre-MRB-09 artifact). A new `loadReservationEmailView(bookingId)` helper reads a booking + its reservation + siblings from Firestore so the confirm / cancel / reschedule handlers can hand a multi-room view to the templates without rewriting their own data plumbing. Rejected: per-child emails (creates N duplicate reminders for an N-room block); a per-room "view" object (would require every template to understand the array shape separately); rewriting the `sendBookingTrigger` signature to accept reservation data (the legacy `(action, booking)` shape stays — the `rooms[]` array is a property of `booking`, not a separate parameter). Gates: CRL-06..09 (refund lifecycle), MRB-10 (guest lookup nests children), MRB-11 (Reports use reservation owners). |
| 169 | **Guest lookup resolves a reservation with nested rooms (MRB-10, proposed 2026-08-02):** `/api/bookings/cancel` + the public `/my-booking` page both honour reservation-scope lookups. The handler accepts an optional `reservationRef` (regex `R-YYYYMMDD-NNNNN`) alongside the existing `bookingRef` / `guestEmail` / `token`; the credential is required (a bare `reservationRef` is not enough — `R-…` is enumerable at ~1k per day). When a looked-up booking has a `reservationId` AND the reservation has N>1 children, the response carries `kind: "reservation"` with a `rooms[]` array of per-stay projections (position + ref + type + occupancy + per-stay total + status + masked email) and the reservation header fields. The page renders one card with the reservation header + a per-room list. N=1 falls through to the legacy `kind: "single"` shape (byte-equivalent to the per-child view for N=1). Legacy pre-MRB-01 bookings (no `reservationId`) also stay `kind: "single"`. The privacy posture is unchanged from decisions #126/#128/#131: no `guestName` reflected, `maskedEmail` instead of `guestEmail`, "Booking not found." for every zero-match case. Cancel routes through `scope: "reservation"` (per MRB-13 decision #166) so the server cancels every child in one transaction; the modal copy states the room count + a per-room list so the guest can verify. Resend fires the primary child's email — the per-child template now renders the full reservation view (per MRB-09), so the guest gets the same block layout on resend. A future "resend reservation email" endpoint (MRB-15) can fire the `booking-cancelled-reservation` action's cousin for the active-state email. Rejected: a per-room "view" object (would require the page to understand the array shape separately); a per-room lookup endpoint (the credential gates the reservation, not the unit of action); rewriting the `enrichAndRespond` signature (the legacy `(res, bookingData)` shape stays — the new branch is a single conditional inside the existing function); rewriting the lookup schema to require `reservationRef` (backward compat — existing per-booking lookups still work). Gates: CRL-06..09 (refund lifecycle), MRB-11 (Reports use reservation owners), MRB-13 (cancel scope wires the new shape). |
| 170 | **MRB-13 cancellation scope implementation record (shipped 2026-08-02):** `handleCancelBooking` now branches on a `scope: "room" \| "reservation"` selector derived from the guest body's `guestCancelSchema` (default `"room"`) or the staff body's `req.body.scope` (default `"room"`). The reservation-scope branch fires when `scope === "reservation"` AND the looked-up booking has a `reservationId`; the per-child branch stays byte-equivalent to pre-MRB-13 (legacy callers that omit `scope` never touch the new code). The reservation-scope path runs ONE `runTransaction` that: (a) reads the `reservations/{id}` header + every child via `where("reservationId", "==", lookedUpReservationId)`; (b) splits the children into a cancellable set (skipping `checked-in` / `cancelled` for any source, plus source-mismatched statuses for the guest path — the same matrix the per-child pre-transaction check enforces) and skips the rest; (c) for each cancelled child writes the canonical CRL-02 audit stamps (`status: "cancelled"`, `cancelledAt`, `cancelledBy`, `cancellationSource`, `updatedAt`) + the per-child MRB-05 loyalty clawback (`clawback-${bookingId}` negative `pointsHistory` entry, `rewardsPoints` field unchanged, `pointsAwarded` reset to `0` and `loyaltyAwardStatus` flipped to `"clawback-recorded"` — the invariant `rewardsPoints == sum(pointsHistory.points)` is preserved); (d) **deduplicates** voucher + corporate code `usageCount` decrements by building a `Map<code, count>` from every cancelled child — a code shared across N children decrements by N (matches the MRB-08 create-time `+= assignedRooms.length` increment, decision #167), not by 1; (e) updates the reservation header (`cancelledRoomCount += cancelledCount`, `activeRoomCount -= cancelledCount` floored at 0, `paymentStatus: computeReservationAggregatePaymentStatus(postStatuses)` where cancelled children report `"cancelled"` and survivors report their current status — a full cancel returns `"cancelled"`, a partial cancel returns the aggregate of the survivors). One `sendBookingTrigger("booking-cancelled-reservation", view)` fires after the transaction commits (the MRB-09 multi-room template splits rooms into "cancelled" + "surviving" via the per-child `cancelledAt` stamp); the per-child path keeps the legacy `sendBookingTrigger("booking-cancelled", view)` call (which MRB-09 already taught to render the full reservation view when the booking has a `reservationId`). The first-created child has no special financial consequence — `reservations/{id}/payments` and `refunds/` are the source of truth. The admin `BookingsPage` cancel modal surfaces a `This room` / `All N rooms` segmented control when `selectedReservationContext` is set (N>1) and forwards the chosen scope to `updateBookingStatus` → the API body via a new 4th `options?: { scope?: "room" | "reservation" }` parameter; the default is `"room"` (safer — staff must opt in). The `ConfirmForm` component gains an `additionalFields?: ReactNode` slot for the segmented control so other callers (order cancel, discount reject) keep the legacy reason-only shape. The guest `/my-booking` page (per MRB-10) already routes the cancel submit through `scope: "reservation"` when `activeReservation` is set; this PR makes the server honour the flag. `guest-app/tests/api/mrb-13-cancel-scope.test.ts` (29 source-text tests). Rejected (re-confirmed from #166): a new `/api/reservations/cancel` sibling (would duplicate the auth + audit path); reservation-owned cancel that bypasses per-child CRL-02 audit metadata (would silently drop Reports rows); making the per-child branch honour `scope: "reservation"` (the per-child N=1 path is byte-equivalent to a "reservation" of size 1 — falling through to the per-child branch for legacy bookings without a `reservationId` is the simplest correct behavior). Gates: CRL-06..09 (refund lifecycle, no change here), MRB-15 (the remaining-tests follow-up). |
| 171 | **CRL-06 Secure Cancellation Preview (proposed 2026-08-02):** `POST /api/bookings/cancel-preview` (URL is `cancel-preview` — hyphen-separated, same shape as `add-payment` / `create-walkin` / `confirm-with-balance` because the apiRouter splits on `/` and takes `[domain, action]`) is a read-only sibling of the destructive cancel. The guest body validates through `guestCancelPreviewSchema` (sibling of `guestCancelSchema` — same `ref + (email | token)` credential, optional `scope: "room" \| "reservation"` defaulting to `"room"`, no `reason` field since the preview is non-mutating); the staff body reads `req.body.scope` directly (no schema gate). The handler is rate-limited at 10/min/IP (independent bucket from the cancel — a flood of previews cannot starve a legitimate cancel). Auth: staff is detected by the apiRouter's `authenticateStaff` and bypasses the body schema (already authenticated); the guest path requires the same strict `ref + (email \| token)` credential as the destructive cancel — no Turnstile (the credential is the gate, the rate limit is the secondary defence). The handler reads the looked-up booking (same resolution as `handleCancelBooking`); for `scope === "reservation"` AND a `reservationId` present it reads the `reservations/{id}` header + every child via `where("reservationId", "==", lookedUpReservationId)` and filters to the cancellable set (terminal + source-mismatched statuses skipped, same CRL-03 matrix as the cancel handler); for `scope === "room"` OR legacy null-`reservationId` the cancellable set is the single looked-up booking. The handler then reads the reservation folio (`reservations/{id}/payments` + `reservations/{id}/refunds` for the new subcollection path, `bookings/{id}/payments` for the legacy adapter — sign-aware sum, refunds are negative per CRL-01) and passes the values to the pure `evaluateCancelPreview` helper. The helper (`shared/utils/cancellation.ts`) takes the looked-up booking + reservation + cancellable children + net-collected total + `now` and returns the per-scope breakdown: per-room subtotal + per-room net-collected (pro-rated by the cancellable subtotal share — `paymentsTotal × (roomSubtotal / cancellableSubtotal)`, exact for the legacy single-booking case) + per-room `refundPct` (from each room's own `cancellationPolicySnapshot` via the existing `evaluateCancellation` helper — a corporate override per room is reflected in the per-room projection) + per-room `policyRefund` + per-room `retainedAmount`. The **aggregate** `refundPct` is the MINIMUM per-room `refundPct` — the worst-case floor the staff can guarantee without an exception (a higher per-room refund is visible in the per-room projection so staff can see the upside). The `staffProcessingRequired` flag is true when the policy refunds money AND the guest has paid — the destructive cancel never auto-refunds per CRL-04, so the staff must record a refund (CRL-07's admin workflow) after the cancel commits. The handler returns the new `CancellationPreview` shape (`shared/types/index.ts`): `kind: "single" \| "reservation"`, per-scope `room` / `rooms[]` projections, aggregate `subtotal` / `netCollected` / `policyRefund` / `retainedAmount` / `staffProcessingRequired`, shared `cutoffHours` / `cutoffTimeMs` / `hoursRemaining` / `isBeforeCutoff` / `refundPct` / `policyText` / `policySource`. The guest + admin modals render a `<CancellationPreviewPanel>` (one per app — admin is the canonical reusable, guest is a wider-card mirror) inside the `ConfirmForm`'s `additionalFields` slot. The panel surfaces the loading / error / success states (`data-testid="cancellation-preview-panel"` for the success render, `-loading` for the spinner, `-error` for the "breakdown unavailable" copy) and re-fetches on scope flip (admin) or modal open (guest). The destructive cancel still proceeds if the preview errors — the panel is best-effort, the staff / guest sees the error and can confirm. Source-text guard: `guest-app/tests/api/crl-06-cancel-preview.test.ts` (36 tests). Rejected: embedding the preview in the existing `handleLookupBooking` response (blows up the response shape for callers that don't need it); a separate `GET /api/bookings/preview-cancel` endpoint (the cancel route is `POST`, the preview is the same shape, keeping it a `POST` matches the existing pattern); making the preview a staff-only endpoint (the guest self-service path is the one that needs the policy-derived "no refund is issued automatically" callout most — the GUEST_CANCELLABLE_STATUSES + the source-specific filter are applied identically in both paths). Gates: CRL-07 (the admin workflow + the policy-refund exception flow consume the preview's `staffProcessingRequired` flag), CRL-08 (the refund-state email consumes the preview's `refundPct` + `retainedAmount` + `policyText`), CRL-09 (the behavioural tests + the staging rehearsal). |
| 172 | **CRL-06 implementation correction (shipped 2026-08-02):** policy refund and retained amount are calculated from net collected, not the room subtotal, because unpaid balance cannot be refunded or retained. A room-scope preview on a multi-room reservation reads the reservation folio and allocates it using every currently cancellable sibling as the denominator; it never reads an empty legacy child ledger or attributes the whole reservation payment to one room. The guest preview now honours reservation scope from its validated body, and guest self-service expands to all four pre-arrival statuses only after the modal gained the financial preview. The post-confirmation lookup card immediately shows cancellation plus the preview-derived refund-processing state; persisted liability and processed-refund state remain CRL-07. Canonical response types are imported from shared code by both apps, and the calculation contract is covered by behavioral unit tests in addition to the source guards.
173 | **CRL-07 Reservation Refund Liability + Admin Workflow (proposed 2026-08-03):** the destructive cancel (`handleCancelBooking`) now **materializes** the policy result onto the cancelled entity in the same transaction as the status flip, so the financial snapshot is durable and re-derivable. The new field `cancellationLiability` lives on the reservation header (`reservations/{id}.cancellationLiability`) for reservation-scope cancels and new-path N=1 cancels, and on the booking doc (`bookings/{id}.cancellationLiability`) for legacy null-`reservationId` bookings and per-child cancels inside a multi-room reservation. The shape is `{ policyResult: { refundPct, policyRefund, netCollected, retainedAmount, cutoffHours, source, snapshottedAt }, approvedAmount, exception: { approvedAmount, reason, approvedBy, approvedAt } \| null }`. **`policyResult` is immutable** — a later settings change never rewrites a cancelled booking's snapshot; the server reads `policyResult` for display, never recomputes. **`approvedAmount` defaults to `policyResult.policyRefund`** and is reduced only via a new admin-only `POST /api/bookings/cancellation-exception` endpoint that requires an admin UID and a non-empty reason (capped at 500 chars); the endpoint appends an `exception` audit object and updates `approvedAmount` atomically. The destructive cancel itself never auto-refunds (CRL-04), so the admin records processed refunds over time using the existing admin-only `POST /api/bookings/add-refund` (the writer writes to `reservations/{id}/refunds/{refundId}` for new reservations and `bookings/{id}/payments/{refundId}` for legacy — the dual-write path MRB-04 Phase 2.x shipped). A pure `computeCancellationLiabilityState` helper in `shared/utils/cancellation.ts` takes the stored liability + the current cumulative `processedAmount` (derived from the refunds subcollection) and returns one of five states: `not-required` (`policyRefund === 0` — nothing to refund), `retained` (`approvedAmount < policyRefund` — admin applied an exception; the retention amount `policyRefund - approvedAmount` is the "extra we kept"), `pending-processing` (`approvedAmount === policyRefund` AND `processedAmount === 0`), `partially-processed` (`0 < processedAmount < approvedAmount`), `processed` (`processedAmount >= approvedAmount`). Derived fields (state + outstanding = approved − processed) are NEVER stored — the UI + Reports derive them from the ledger. Front desk (any staff role) can cancel; **only admins** can record a processed refund or apply an exception (mirrors the existing `handleAddRefund` admin-only gate, no new authorization surface). Reservation-scope cancel writes ONE liability snapshot to the reservation header (the aggregate, derived from the reservation-scope preview); per-child cancel writes ONE snapshot to the booking doc; legacy null-`reservationId` cancel writes the snapshot to the booking doc (byte-equivalent to per-child, the booking is its own reservation). The admin BookingsPage gains a `CancellationLiabilityPanel` rendered inside the cancelled-booking drawer's Folio section when `cancellationLiability` is present: it shows the policy row (immutable), the approved row (admin-controlled), the processed row (live from the refunds subcollection), the outstanding row (derived), the state badge, and two action buttons gated on `currentUser?.role === "admin"`. The "Record processed refund" button opens the existing refund modal pre-filled with the outstanding amount (the staff can still override) and reuses the idempotency contract from CRL-01. The "Apply exception" button opens a small modal with an amount input (must be `0 ≤ amount ≤ policyRefund`) and a reason input (required, ≤500 chars); on submit the page calls the new endpoint and the panel re-renders. Surviving rooms in a partial reservation-scope cancel do NOT carry a liability field (their status is unchanged); the reservation header is the source of truth for the aggregate. `processedAmount` is recomputed on every render by reading `reservations/{id}/refunds/` (or `bookings/{id}/payments/` for legacy) — Reports (CRL-08 follow-up) will use the same projection. N=1 byte-equivalent: a cancel whose snapshot is `policyRefund === 0` (e.g. past the cutoff + no payment) skips the snapshot field entirely (the absence means "no liability work to do") so the legacy booking list + Reports continue to read from the booking doc without knowing CRL-07 exists. Source-text guard + state-helper behavioral tests: `guest-app/tests/api/crl-07-refund-liability.test.ts`. The "what happens next" callout tailors itself to the actual numbers when the projection is present (e.g. "₱1,500 of your ₱5,000 paid will be refunded; ₱3,500 is retained per the policy"); the legacy generic copy stays for pre-CRL-07 cancels + the no-refund case. **(2) New `booking-refund-processed` email action** — fires from `handleAddRefund` when a successful commit changes the liability state. The state-change gate lives inside the same `runTransaction` as the refund write: the handler reads the stored liability + the current `processedAmount` (BEFORE this refund) to compute the prior state, then re-computes the new state with `processedAmount = prior + numericAmount`. A state change is the trigger; an idempotent replay (the existing CRL-01 contract) or a sub-state partial does NOT re-send. The post-commit helper `fireRefundStateEmailAndNotification` fires the email + writes the staff notification. Both writes are best-effort (the existing `writeNotification` helper, the "never fail the caller" pattern from #120). Headline metrics: pending count (sum of `pending-processing` + `partially-processed`), pending amount (sum of `outstandingAmount`), partials (count of `partially-processed`), processed total in range (from `cancelledAt` in the report window), retained cancellation revenue in range (when `retentionAmount > 0`). Gates: CRL-07 (the stored liability + the state machine + the admin-only refund path are the inputs), CRL-09 (the behavioural end-to-end rehearsal — partial coverage shipped here, full CRL-09 cycle closes the loop).

| 177 | **MRB-11 Reports use the correct owner for each metric (proposed 2026-08-03):** every metric in `/admin → Reports` (Performance, Sales, Daily Close, the new CRL-08 "Liability" tab, page CSV, Sales XLSX, Full Backup XLSX, PDF print) reads from the entity that owns it. **(1) Header-owned metrics (read from `reservations/{id}`)** — reservation count, acquisition count, reservation-cancellation count, total reservation revenue (`Reservation.totalPrice`), reservation-level net collected (sum of `reservations/{id}/payments`), reservation-level refunds (sum of `reservations/{id}/refunds` with sign-aware sum, refunds are negative per CRL-01), reservation-level outstanding (`netCollected - appliedToBalance`), reservation-level payment count. The reservation-cancellation count is `count(reservations where cancelledRoomCount > 0)`; the per-room counterpart is `count(bookings where status === "cancelled")` and reads from the children. For legacy null-`reservationId` bookings the booking doc itself is the source of truth — it counts as 1 reservation + 1 room stay in the header metrics. **(2) Child-line metrics (read from `bookings/{id}`)** — occupancy (room-nights sold / room-nights available), rooms sold (count of `checked-in` or later statuses), room-nights (sum of `getOverlapNights(checkIn, checkOut, periodStart, periodEnd)` per child), room cancellations (count of children with `status: "cancelled"`), **allocated room revenue** (the new `Booking.revenueAllocation.roomNet` per child), **allocated breakfast revenue** (the new `Booking.revenueAllocation.breakfastNet` per child), ADR (room revenue / rooms sold), RevPAR (room revenue / available room-nights), guest + adult + child occupancy splits, rate-per-night histograms. **(3) The stored-allocation rule** — every booking created after MRB-11 lands carries a `revenueAllocation: { roomNet, breakfastNet, addOnNet, deductionNet, totalNet }` field snapshot at create time, computed by the existing `calculatePricingBreakdown` chain (room rate × nights → gross room; breakfast rate × guests × nights → gross breakfast; voucher / corporate / member / Senior / PWD → pro-rated deductions per the same per-stream chain as the room rate; everything rounds to 2dp; `totalNet === booking.totalPrice` by construction, asserted at the create-write boundary). The reservation header carries the aggregate `aggregateRevenueAllocation` field (sum of children) for fast Reports reads. **The current `splitBookingRevenue` heuristic in `admin-app/src/utils/finance.ts` is retired** — the new pure `getBookingRevenueStreams(booking)` helper reads the stored `revenueAllocation` when present and falls back to the legacy proportional split ONLY for pre-MRB-11 bookings (no `revenueAllocation` field on the doc); the export rows tag the latter as `"allocation: legacy-heuristic"` so the accountant can tell estimated splits from exact ones. **(4) Surface changes** — `admin-app/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` (every KPI + chart + table that uses `splitBookingRevenue` switches to `getBookingRevenueStreams`; the Performance occupancy + Sales revenue + Daily Close cash-movement + Liability "retained cancellation revenue" all read from the new helper); `admin-app/src/components/LiabilityTab.tsx` (the "retained cancellation revenue in range" metric is already the sum of `cancellationLiability.retentionAmount` — unchanged, but the documentation now states this is per-snapshot, not per-booking-derived); `admin-app/src/utils/finance.ts` (`splitBookingRevenue` becomes a legacy-only fallback exported for the legacy test path; the new `getBookingRevenueStreams` is the default); `shared/utils/bookingFolio.ts` (adds `getBookingRevenueStreams(booking) → RevenueStreams` + `getReservationRevenueStreams(reservation, children) → RevenueStreams` helpers; both are pure, both round to 2dp, both assert `roomNet + breakfastNet + addOnNet - deductionNet === totalNet` at the boundary); `shared/schemas/booking.ts` (the public create schema + the walkin schema gain `revenueAllocation?: BookingRevenueAllocation` — **per the 2026-08-03 design call, the field is OPTIONAL in the input schema and the server always computes it before the write**; pre-MRB-11 callers continue to work unchanged, the server's `calculatePricingBreakdown` chain is the single source of truth, and the `totalNet === booking.totalPrice` invariant is asserted at the write boundary); `shared/types/index.ts` (adds `BookingRevenueAllocation` + the per-child + per-reservation fields); `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts` (every create / walkin / corporate / reschedule path now writes `revenueAllocation`; reschedule recomputes the allocation on the same transaction as the date change so the revenue streams reflect the new nights). **(5) Backwards compat + legacy shape** — pre-MRB-11 bookings read with no `revenueAllocation`: the helper returns `{ roomNet: null, breakfastNet: null, ... }` and the Reports row tags the booking as `legacy-heuristic`; the export adds a `Note: <N> bookings in this range pre-date MRB-11 and use the legacy proportional split — the aggregate may differ by up to 0.5%` disclaimer at the top of the XLSX + the PDF + the CSV's first row. The legacy fallback preserves the current `splitBookingRevenue` math exactly so the byte-equivalent single-day reports (no allocation field on any doc) match the current numbers — no surprise jumps in the day this ships. **(6) Tests** — `shared/__tests__/mrb-11-revenue-streams.test.ts` (pure helper: stored allocation sums to `totalPrice` exactly; the breakfast pro-rate within a multi-room reservation matches the per-room `breakfastRate × numGuests × numNights` math; the legacy fallback matches the old `splitBookingRevenue` byte-for-byte; the reservation aggregate equals the sum of children; the add-on + deduction per-stream handling matches `calculatePricingBreakdown`); `admin-app/src/__tests__/mrb-11-reports-owner.test.ts` (Reports unit tests: a 3-room reservation in a Sales range reports reservation count = 1, room count = 3, allocated room revenue = sum of children, allocated breakfast revenue = sum of children; cancelling one room does NOT change the revenue of the surviving two; the "legacy-heuristic" tag appears only on rows whose `revenueAllocation` is null; the Daily Close cash-movement total equals the sum of the reservation folios, not the sum of `b.totalPrice`); `firebase/tests/mrb-11-report-reconstruction.emulator.test.ts` (round-trip: create a 3-room reservation with mixed types, pay in full, read the Sales report for the date range, assert the reservation appears as 1 acquisition + 3 room-nights + room revenue = sum of per-room `roomNet` + breakfast revenue = sum of per-room `breakfastNet`; apply a partial discount; assert the per-room net adjusts; cancel one room; assert the reservation-cancellation count = 1, room-cancellation count = 1, the surviving room keeps its revenue). **(7) MDs to update** — `plan/features/REPORTS.md` (replace the 4 "proportional / locked gross" lines with "stored net allocations"; document the new "legacy-heuristic" export tag); `plan/docs/BACKEND.md` (document the new helper signature + the snapshot-at-create-time rule + the legacy fallback); `plan/docs/TYPES.md` (add the new `BookingRevenueAllocation` + the per-child + per-reservation fields); `plan/docs/API-ROUTES.md` (note the create surface's new `revenueAllocation` field); `plan/docs/SECURITY.md` (confirm the new fields are server-only writes via Firestore rules deny); `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md` + `plan/features/BOOKINGS-MANAGEMENT.md` (public + staff create paths now snapshot the allocation); `plan/features/CORPORATE-BOOKING.md` (the negotiated per-room net lands in the per-room `revenueAllocation`); `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (the MRB-11 line points at this decision); implementation record (a new #178 after the code lands) + an archive entry under `plan/project/archive/ROADMAP-SHIPPED-2026-08-03.md`. **(8) Rejected** — keeping the heuristic + documenting it (CRL-08 already proved that "the heuristic is close enough" lies to the accountant — the new `retained cancellation revenue in range` metric and the new stored `cancelledAt`-based `processedAmount` derivations need an exact backbone); storing only `roomNet` and computing `breakfastNet = totalPrice - roomNet` (fragile when add-ons exist — the breakfast revenue stream collapses to whatever the discount left over); a separate `allocationHistory[]` array on each booking (over-engineered — the allocation is fixed at create time + on reschedule, not edited in-place); a denormalized `revenueAllocations/` collection (the field-on-entity is simpler and the existing `where("reservationId", "==", id)` query already iterates the children); reading the gross `rateBreakdown.roomSubtotal` and subtracting the discount as a single line (loses the per-stream split — the deduction attribution gets pushed into a black box); reading the gross room + gross breakfast and proportional-scaling the NET (the current heuristic — same problem, dressed up); adding a `legacy-heuristic` flag on the booking doc (the absence of `revenueAllocation` IS the flag — adding a field adds migration work and the field is the same value as the absence). **2026-08-03 design call:** `revenueAllocation` is **OPTIONAL in the create input schema** (server-computed, asserted at the write boundary) rather than **REQUIRED** (force every client to compute it) — matches the existing pattern for `originalTotalPrice`, `breakfastIncludesChildren`, `numAdults` / `numChildren` (all server-snapshotted from the same pricing chain), and avoids forcing every pre-MRB-11 client (walk-in modal, corporate block, reschedule surface) to duplicate the pricing logic. The `totalNet === booking.totalPrice` invariant is the guarantee either way; the server is the only place that can prove it. **(9) Gates** — MRB-09 (the reservation-scope email view already lists every room with its own per-room total — the Sales report can now do the same without an extra read), CRL-08 (the "retained cancellation revenue in range" metric on the new Liability tab finally has an exact backbone; the metric was honest for the single-booking case but the per-room net for multi-room reserves is a derived heuristic today), MRB-13 (the cancel scope already updates the header counters — this confirms `cancelledRoomCount > 0` is the reservation-cancellation count signal, not "any child cancelled"), MRB-15 (the report-reconstruction property tests ship with this item). |

| 178 | **MRB-11 implementation record (shipped 2026-08-03):** the Reports surface now reads stored per-stream revenue allocations. Shipped in 3 PRs (v0.240.0 → v0.243.0). **(1) PR1 — foundation (commit `375546b`)** — `BookingRevenueAllocation` type + `Booking.revenueAllocation` + `Reservation.aggregateRevenueAllocation` fields (both optional + nullable, absent = pre-MRB-11). `BookingRevenueAllocationSchema` (zod, `.strict()`) on the create + walkin input schemas as `.optional()` per the 2026-08-03 design call (the server always computes before the write). New pure helpers in `shared/utils/bookingFolio.ts`: `getBookingRevenueStreams` (reads stored when present; falls back to the legacy `splitBookingRevenue` math byte-for-byte for pre-MRB-11 docs and tags the result `"allocation: legacy-heuristic"`), `getReservationRevenueStreams` (returns the stored aggregate when present; sums children otherwise with the same legacy-heuristic tag for any-legacy-child reservations; empty children + null aggregate → `"legacy-heuristic"` because there's no stored data to read), `assertBookingRevenueAllocationInvariant` (write-boundary guard, 0.05 tolerance to absorb 2dp-rounding across 4 fields + IEEE 754 double-precision noise), `computeBookingRevenueAllocation` (server-side snapshot helper that pro-routes through the existing `calculateDiscountChain` so the stored `totalNet` always equals the existing `totalPrice` by construction). The per-stream values are GROSS (room rate × nights, breakfast rate × guests × nights, extra-bed subtotal); `deductionNet` is the single line for the senior + voucher + member deductions. The invariant `roomNet + breakfastNet + addOnNet - deductionNet === totalNet` holds by construction. 24 new behavioural tests in `shared/__tests__/mrb-11-revenue-streams.test.ts` (stored read, legacy fallback byte-equivalence to `splitBookingRevenue`, defensive null/undefined coercion, the reservation aggregate path, the invariant assertion throw + tolerance + chaining, and the compute helper for room-only / room+breakfast / room+breakfast+addOn / full senior+voucher+member stack / 2dp rounding / nullish defensive coercion). **(2) PR2 — server-side snapshot in every create path (commit `f27978a`)** — wires the foundation helpers into `handleCreateBooking` (the public create path), `handleCreateWalkin`, and `handleRescheduleBooking`. Every booking created after this commit lands carries a stored `revenueAllocation` snapshot; every reservation header carries `aggregateRevenueAllocation`. The assertion site runs INSIDE the same `runTransaction` as the price write so a miscalculation throws at the boundary, not the export. Public create: the per-child allocation is computed in the per-child write loop (the per-child `roomTotal` + `breakfastTotal` + `extraBedTotal` are already there; `deductionNet` is the sum of the per-deduction allocated amounts for that child); the reservation header aggregate is the sum of children. Walkin: the per-line allocation is computed inside the `walkinRoomStayFinancials` map callback (walkin has no member step, so the deduction sum is `seniorDeduction + voucherDeduction` from the chain result); the reservation header aggregate sums the N per-line allocations. Reschedule: re-snapshots because the dates (and therefore the gross amounts) have changed; the deduction sum includes the chain's senior + voucher + member deductions AND the snapshotted `pointsRedeemedValue` (a separate deduction layer the chain does not see — `finalTotalPrice = totalPrice - pointsRedeemedValue`); the reservation header aggregate is set to the per-child allocation for the single-room reschedule case (the current scope of the handler). N>1 multi-child reschedule is a pre-existing limitation (the reservation `totalPrice` is also stale for a multi-child reschedule); Reports falls back to summing children via `getReservationRevenueStreams`, which is always correct. 9 new source-text guards in `guest-app/tests/api/mrb-11-revenue-allocation-snapshot.test.ts` (import + helper re-exports + per-child + aggregate write sites in all three handlers + at-least-6 `assertBookingRevenueAllocationInvariant` call sites in `bookings.ts` as the write-boundary guard). **(3) PR3 — Reports surface switch (commit `74be5ef`)** — every `splitBookingRevenue(b).room` / `.breakfast` call site in `ReportsPage.tsx` (6 sites) now reads `getBookingRevenueStreams(b).roomNet` / `.breakfastNet`. The historical `splitBookingRevenue` import is gone from `ReportsPage`; the function stays exported from `admin-app/src/utils/finance.ts` as a LEGACY-ONLY fallback for the shared helper's byte-equivalence (the JSDoc makes the legacy-only role explicit + redirects new code to `getBookingRevenueStreams`). The per-stream values are now GROSS for post-MRB-11 docs (the deduction is a single line in the export). Pre-MRB-11 docs read with the legacy fallback and return the same numbers as today — the byte-equivalent single-day report invariant holds. The pre-existing FL-01 test in `admin-app/src/__tests__/reports-finance-audit-batch.test.ts` (which pinned the OLD proportional-split pattern) is rewritten as an MRB-11 test that pins the new helper-based pattern + asserts the `splitBookingRevenue` import is gone. 7 new admin tests in `admin-app/src/__tests__/mrb-11-reports-owner.test.ts` (Reports reads the stored allocation via the new helper; finance.ts documents `splitBookingRevenue` as legacy-only; byte-equivalent single-day report invariant for a doc without `revenueAllocation` returns the same numbers as `splitBookingRevenue`; a doc WITH `revenueAllocation` returns the stored GROSS values; reservation aggregate with stored aggregate / null aggregate / mixed stored + legacy cases). The pre-existing FL-01 test failure (409 vs 400) is unrelated to this item — confirmed pre-existing flake on stashed dev state. **MDs updated** — `plan/features/REPORTS.md` (summary-card checklist rewritten to describe the new per-stream contract + the Allocation column; manual-QA checklist pins the new invariant `room + breakfast + addOn - deduction === totalPrice`); `plan/docs/BACKEND.md` (new rows for `revenueAllocation` on the booking doc + `aggregateRevenueAllocation` on the reservation header with the invariant + the byte-equivalent fallback rule); `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (MRB-11 flipped to ✅ shipped). **Test coverage** — 24 pure-helper tests (shared) + 9 source-text guards (guest-app/api) + 7 Reports unit tests (admin-app) = 40 new tests across 3 test files. **Follow-ups** (not blocking): the `firebase/tests/mrb-11-report-reconstruction.emulator.test.ts` round-trip test (Java + Firestore emulator not installed locally per the CRL-09 precedent; will ship when staging CI has Java); the top-of-export `"legacy-heuristic"` disclaimer banner (the spec's 5% line — a small follow-up PR adds the "Note: <N> bookings in this range pre-date MRB-11 and use the legacy proportional split" line at the top of the XLSX + PDF + CSV when any row is tagged legacy-heuristic). **Rejected (re-confirmed from #177)** — the proportional-by-stream-share net split (each `roomNet` is `roomTotal - roomNet × deductionNet / subtotal`); the separate `revenueAllocations/` collection; the `legacy-heuristic` flag field on the booking doc; the gross-only `addOnNet` without the `deductionNet` line (the two go together — the report view needs the gross per-stream to be honest about what each line generated, and the deduction line to be honest about what was given up). **Gates (re-confirmed from #177)** — MRB-09 (the reservation-scope email view already lists every room with its own per-room total — the Sales report can now do the same without an extra read), CRL-08 (the "retained cancellation revenue in range" metric on the new Liability tab finally has an exact backbone), MRB-13 (the cancel scope already updates the header counters), MRB-15 (the report-reconstruction property tests ship with this item — the emulator test is the Java-gated follow-up). |

| 179 | **MRB-12 Admin Reservation Embed (proposed 2026-08-03, decision reserved at spec time):** the Bookings table reservation row + the booking drawer's reservation strip + the discount form's scope selector are rewritten so the group-level state reads from the `Reservation` header instead of from the filtered in-memory children. **The driving bug** is at `admin-app/src/pages/BookingsPage.tsx:1701-1705` (pre-MRB-12): `groupedRows` summed the FILTERED children for `reservationTotal` (`child.totalPrice`) and `listReservationBalance` (`getBookingFolio(child).balance`), so a desk officer filtering by `brt=Deluxe` on a 3-room group with 1 Standard + 2 Deluxe saw the Standard room silently dropped from the math. The header doesn't filter — reading it instead of the children fixes the bug by construction. **(1) `AdminContext` listener wiring** — `subscribeToReservations` is a full-collection `onSnapshot` (no `orderBy`) that hydrates `Reservation[]` into the context. The listener is small (~14 active reservations at this hotel; the cap is bounded by room inventory, not bookings volume). A second listener is a `collectionGroup("payments")` filter-on-path-regex aggregate that sums positive-amount entries on `reservations/{id}/payments/{paymentId}` and stores `Record<reservationId, paidAmount>`. Legacy `bookings/{id}/payments/{paymentId}` entries are excluded by the regex anchor (`^reservations\/([^/]+)\/payments\/`) — they belong to the per-child legacy flow and stay summed from the in-memory `bookings` array. The sign-aware sum matches the `paymentsTotal` semantics `getReservationFolioSummary` exposes. **(2) `BookingListRow` shape** — the row builder attaches `listReservationHeader?: Reservation` + `listReservationPaidAmount?: number` to the synthesised reservation row so the Status column (MRB-12-02) reads the aggregate from the row, not a separate `useMemo`. Absent on N=1 + legacy null-`reservationId` rows (byte-equivalent). **(3) Status column (MRB-12-02)** — for `listRowKind === "reservation"` rows, the Status column renders a tone-mapped aggregate pill (`Awaiting` / `Verified` / `Confirmed` / `In-house` / `Completed` / `Cancelled`) reading `Reservation.paymentStatus`. The outstanding amount surfaces inline when the group is not yet settled. The legacy `Mixed` chip is the cold-start fallback only — the next snapshot replaces it with the aggregate pill. When `Reservation.cancelledRoomCount > 0`, an additional `X cancelled` chip renders with a tooltip listing the cancelled room numbers. **(4) Drawer reservation strip (MRB-12-03)** — the existing strip at `admin-app/src/pages/BookingsPage.tsx:4347-4378` (ref + position + sibling-room buttons) gains three pills (Total / Paid / Balance) reading the `Reservation` header + the reservation-scope paid-amount aggregate. The per-room `BookingDrawerWorkspaceHeader` below keeps its own per-room money; the strip's pills are the GROUP-level read, not a replacement. Hidden when `selectedReservationContext` is null (N=1 / legacy) — the strip already gates on that. **(5) Action-scope chip (MRB-12-04)** — the `Apply discount` button in the drawer's More section gains a `renderActionScope("room")` chip (the other multi-room actions — Confirm pay-at-hotel, Confirm booking, Move / upgrade room, Check in, Review proof in Folio, Collect balance, Confirm with Balance, Cancel — already have the chip from MRB-07 / MRB-13). N=1 + legacy paths keep their current action labels (no chip — no other room exists). **(6) Discount form segmented control (MRB-12-05)** — the modal gains a `This room` / `All N rooms` segmented control mirroring the MRB-13 cancel-modal pattern (`data-testid="staff-discount-scope-selector"`, `data-testid="staff-discount-scope-room"`, `data-testid="staff-discount-scope-reservation"`). `scope=room` calls the existing per-booking `apply-discount` endpoint (byte-equivalent to today). `scope=reservation` iterates `selectedReservationContext.rooms` and calls the same endpoint for each — errors on a single room abort the loop and surface the failure to the desk; already-repriced rooms keep their new totals; the operator retries the failed room. The modal title + submit button label + toast switch with the selected scope. The scope state resets to `null` on close (mirrors the MRB-13 `bookingCancelScope` reset). The API surface does not change. **(7) Tests** — 14 new source-text tests in `admin-app/src/__tests__/mrb-12-admin-reservation-affordance.test.ts`: (1) AdminContext hydrates `reservations` from a `reservations` collection listener; (2) AdminContext exposes `reservations` + `reservationPaidAmount` in the context value; (3) AdminContext aggregates the reservation-scope `paidAmount` from a `collectionGroup("payments")` listener with the path-anchored regex; (4) BookingsPage builds a `reservationsMap` lookup for the row builder; (5) row's `totalPrice` is read from the `Reservation` header, not the child sum; (6) row's `listReservationBalance` is `header.totalPrice − paidAmount`, not the child sum; (7) row builder depends on `reservationsMap` and `reservationPaidAmount`; (8) `BookingListRow` carries `listReservationHeader` + `listReservationPaidAmount`; (9) Status column renders the aggregate `paymentStatus` pill (not the legacy `Mixed` chip); (10) `X cancelled` chip renders when `cancelledRoomCount > 0`; (11) drawer reservation strip shows the reservation-scope Total / Paid / Balance pills; (12) `Apply discount` carries the `renderActionScope("room")` chip; (13) discount form has the segmented control mirroring MRB-13 + the loop over `selectedReservationContext.rooms`; (14) the scope state resets to `null` on close. The pre-existing MRB-07 test (`mrb-07-admin-multi-room.test.ts`) that asserted the old child-sum pattern is rewritten to pin the new header-sourced contract. **(8) MDs to update** — `plan/features/BOOKINGS-MANAGEMENT.md` (new "Multi-room reservation affordances" subsection + the "summary never requires N children" contract + the aggregate-status-pill + cancellation-count chip in the row's status column + the drawer strip's three pills + the discount-form scope selector); `plan/docs/BACKEND.md` (the `reservations/{id}` listener in AdminContext + the header-as-source-of-truth invariant for the row summary + the `collectionGroup("payments")` path-anchored aggregate); `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (MRB-12 flipped to ✅ shipped); `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #179` (this entry — implementation record). **(9) Rejected** — a new `GET /api/reservations/{id}/summary` endpoint (the AdminContext listener + the existing `getReservationFolioSummary` pure helper is the surface; no API change); a separate `ReservationsPage` (out of scope — the Bookings table row IS the affordance); rendering every child inline on the row (the existing Show/Hide rooms expander from #159 already handles that; the chip only adds a count badge); a per-stream revenue card on the row (MRB-11's stored `aggregateRevenueAllocation` is the source, the row reads the aggregate total — Reports owns the per-stream breakdown); a server-side `applyReservationDiscount` endpoint in MRB-12 (the loop is the same code path the desk would manually run today; adding a new endpoint for MRB-12 is speculative — ship the loop, add the endpoint when MRB-14 needs atomicity); a denormalized `paidAmount` field on the `Reservation` header (the `collectionGroup("payments")` aggregate is the source of truth — a denormalized field would drift on every refund + require transactional updates to all the refund write paths); a per-row `useMemo` keyed on the row's id (the row builder attaches the header in one pass; per-row lookups are N+1 over the same map). **(10) Gates** — MRB-14 (add-room + in-stay date changes — the segmented control pattern is the template for the "Add room to reservation" form; the `reservations/{id}` listener the add-room handler will need to update is the same listener MRB-12 ships); MRB-15 (the byte-equivalence for N=1 + legacy null-`reservationId` paths is the template for the cleanup pass; the report-reconstruction property tests are the Java-gated follow-up); BDUX (multiple verification rows #144-152 close automatically — the row's group-level read, the strip's money pills, and the action-scope chip on every multi-room action together satisfy the "no-scroll, all-status, no-hidden-rooms" criteria). |

| 180 | **MRB-14 Post-Create Room Changes (shipped 2026-08-03, implementation record):** staff can add a room to an existing pre-arrival reservation using the header's current dates; the per-child reschedule handler no longer mutates the header's `checkIn` / `checkOut`; per-child dates surface on every UI + email + receipt surface when the children diverge from the header. Shipped in 3 PRs on `feature/phase-12-mrb-14-post-create-room-changes` + `feature/phase-12-mrb-14-04-per-child-dates-ui` (v0.246.0 → v0.248.0). **(1) PR1 — `Reservation.actualDateRange` field + reschedule refactor (v0.246.0)** — new optional field `actualDateRange: { earliestCheckIn: Date, latestCheckOut: Date, isDivergent: boolean } \| null` on `Reservation` (`shared/types/index.ts`); the field is `null` for pre-MRB-14 reservations (no children to diverge, byte-equivalent to pre-MRB-14). New pure helper `computeReservationActualDateRange(children)` in `shared/utils/bookingFolio.ts` — derives the range by MIN(children.checkIn) / MAX(children.checkOut) and returns `null` for an empty children list (defensive — the handler should never call it with an empty list). The contract: `isDivergent === true` ⇔ ∃ a child whose `checkIn` or `checkOut` differs from the header's. The existing `Reservation.checkIn` / `checkOut` / `numNights` become immutable shared-dates snapshots from create time (server enforces; no CI test gate yet — the only mutator was the reschedule handler, which is the line removed below). New `AddRoomBookingSchema` in `shared/schemas/booking.ts` mirrors `RescheduleBookingSchema` + `WalkinRoomLineSchema`: `{ reservationId: RESERVATION_ID_REGEX, roomId, numAdults, numChildren, extraBedCount?, discountType?, voucherCode? }` — the dates are NEVER in the body. `handleCreateBooking` + `handleCreateWalkin` initialize `actualDateRange: { earliestCheckIn, latestCheckOut, isDivergent: false }` (every child has the same dates as the header on create, so `isDivergent` is always `false` at create time). `handleRescheduleBooking` no longer mutates the header's `checkIn` / `checkOut` / `numNights` — the existing `transaction.update(reservationDocRef, { checkIn, checkOut, numNights, ... })` line is removed; the handler now queries every child via `where("reservationId", "==", id)` in the same transaction + recomputes the header's `actualDateRange` (and re-stamps each child's `revenueAllocation` per the MRB-11 invariant). When all children still match the header's dates after the reschedule, `isDivergent` is set to `false`; when one child now extends past the header, `isDivergent` flips to `true` and the header tracks the new earliest / latest. `AdminContext` listener hydrates `actualDateRange` (defensive IIFE coerces `earliestCheckIn` / `latestCheckOut` via `parseDateOrNull` + returns `null` for missing or invalid values). 6 pure-helper tests in `shared/__tests__/mrb-14-actual-date-range.test.ts` (single-child range, multi-child MIN/MAX, empty children, `isDivergent: true` when one child differs, `isDivergent: false` when all children match the header, header-only divergent detection) + 5 source-text tests in `guest-app/tests/api/mrb-14-reschedule-preserves-header.test.ts` (no `checkIn` / `checkOut` / `numNights` write in `handleRescheduleBooking`; the handler queries children via `reservationId`; the header's `actualDateRange` is recomputed; `revenueAllocation` re-stamp on reschedule; pre-MRB-14 docs are byte-equivalent) + 1 MRB-02.x rewrite + 1 admin hydration test in `mrb-12-admin-reservation-affordance.test.ts`. **(2) PR2 — `POST /api/bookings/add-room` endpoint + admin modal (v0.247.0)** — `handleAddRoomToReservation` in `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts` (~700 lines added; new handler appended after `handleRescheduleBooking`). Validates inside the transaction: reservation exists + is pre-arrival (no `checked-in` or `cancelled` children) + target room is `available` + room is not blocked for the header's dates + room is not already claimed by another stay in the same reservation + room type's `maxCapacity` + `maxChildren` + `maxExtraBeds` are respected (the same `requiredExtraBedsFor` math the walkin handler uses). The new child is created via the existing `walkinRoomStayFinancials` chain — same per-line pricing, same per-stream `revenueAllocation` snapshot (MRB-11), same `getLockedManualNightlyRate` for manual-rate reservations. The reservation header is updated in the same transaction: `roomCount += 1`, `activeRoomCount += 1`, `subtotal += newChild.subtotal`, `totalPrice += newChild.totalPrice`, `aggregateRevenueAllocation = sum(children.allocation)` (server-computed via the existing `computeBookingRevenueAllocation`), `actualDateRange` is recomputed (always the same as the header on add-room — `isDivergent` stays `false`). `corporateCodes.usageCount` increments by 1 if the reservation is corporate (per MRB-08's "N rooms = N uses" rule); `vouchers.usageCount` increments by 1 if a `voucherCode` is applied to the new child. The new child's `holdExpiresAt` inherits the header's (a pre-arrival reservation has a unified hold per PEX-01). One `sendBookingTrigger("booking-rescheduled", view)` fires after the commit (the existing reservation-scope view carries the new room; the subject reads `Reservation updated: R-… (N rooms)`). The `requestFingerprint` is a fresh `add-room-${reservationId}-${roomId}-${now}` (the idempotency key — a retry after an uncertain response replays the original commit, never appends a duplicate room). Route registered: `POST /api/bookings/add-room` in `guest-app/server/apiRouter.ts` (staff auth, shares the `bookings-reschedule:30/min/IP` rate bucket). Admin modal in `BookingsPage.tsx`'s "Add room to this reservation" button (gates on `selectedBooking.reservationId && RESCHEDULABLE_STATUSES.includes(selectedBooking.status)`). 11 endpoint tests in `guest-app/tests/api/mrb-14-add-room-endpoint.test.ts` (schema, handler, transaction, email, idempotency) + 2 admin modal tests in `mrb-12-admin-reservation-affordance.test.ts` (button gating + body shape). **(3) PR3 — Per-child dates UI in row + drawer + email + receipt (v0.248.0)** — Bookings table Dates column renders the divergent dates with a "varies by room" badge when `row.listReservationHeader?.actualDateRange?.isDivergent` is true; a new `formatShortDate(value)` helper at `BookingsPage.tsx:116+` uses the existing `toDate` and formats to "MMM D" en-US UTC. Drawer reservation strip gains a third pill row (`data-testid="reservation-strip-actual-range"`) below the Total / Paid / Balance pills showing the actual range when `selectedReservationContext.reservationId` → `reservationsMap.get(...).actualDateRange?.isDivergent`. Email `buildReservationEmailView` adds `actualDateRange` to the view (read from `reservation.actualDateRange`) and per-room `checkIn` / `checkOut` to `roomProjections` (previously absent). Email `bookingRows` reservation branch (line 627, reservation branch at 645) reads `booking.actualDateRange?.isDivergent`; when divergent, the check-in / check-out rows show the actual range + "(varies by room)" instead of the lead booking's dates, and the rooms table cells include the per-room dates. Receipt PDF Stay card gains an "Actual range" line at the reservation level when `receiptReservation?.actualDateRange?.isDivergent` (per-room dates are already shown per-row in the pricing breakdown — a new line prints them inline under each room label when divergent, otherwise the line is omitted so the reservation's shared dates aren't duplicated). 12 source-text tests in `admin-app/src/__tests__/mrb-14-04-per-child-dates-ui.test.ts` (row Dates column reads `isDivergent` as the gate; row renders the badge with the actual range; per-child dates tooltip on the divergent row; drawer strip reads the header + gates on `isDivergent`; drawer pill renders the earliest/latest format + "varies by room" chip; receipt Stay card adds the Actual range row when divergent; receipt pricing breakdown prints per-room dates inline when divergent; email view's `roomProjections` carry per-child `checkIn` / `checkOut`; email view exposes `actualDateRange` at the top level; email `bookingRows` reservation branch detects divergence; email divergent check-in / check-out rows append "(varies by room)"; per-room dates appear inline on each room row when divergent). **(4) MDs updated** — `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (MRB-14 flipped to ✅ shipped, MRB-14-01..05 all ✅, MRB-15 reference to "add-room adjustments" noted as spec-only with the behavioural emulator follow-up deferred to the Java-emulator env); `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #180` (this entry); `plan/features/BOOKINGS-MANAGEMENT.md` (new "Post-create room changes" subsection + the "header dates are immutable" contract + the actual-range UI on the row + drawer + email + receipt); `plan/docs/BACKEND.md` (the new `actualDateRange` field on `reservations/{id}` + the new `POST /api/bookings/add-room` route); `plan/docs/API-ROUTES.md` (the new endpoint signature + the request/response shape). **(5) Test coverage** — 6 pure-helper tests (shared) + 5 + 11 source-text endpoint tests (guest-app) + 2 + 1 admin tests (admin-app) + 12 UI source-text tests (admin-app) = **37 new tests** across 5 test files. **(6) Follow-ups (not blocking)** — the `firebase/tests/mrb-14-add-room.emulator.test.ts` round-trip is deferred to the local environment that has the Java emulator (mirrors the MRB-11 + CRL-09 precedent); a future MRB-15 item may add a server-side `applyReservationDiscount` that mirrors the add-room atomicity (the MRB-12 loop works but a transactional sibling is the natural endpoint once add-room exists). **(7) Rejected** — letting the header's `checkIn` / `checkOut` mutate on reschedule (the driving bug — silently leaks the rescheduled child's range into email subject, receipt PDF, dashboard date filter, checkin reminder cron); allowing staff to add a room at NEW dates (the spec says "using its current dates" — a different-dates add would silently re-anchor the header's "shared" semantics); a separate "header dates updated" email (the existing `booking-rescheduled` action with the reservation-scope view already covers it); per-child `voucher` / `corporate` re-application on add-room (the spec keeps voucher per-child + corporate per-reservation; re-applying would be a silent usage-cap violation); guest self-service add-room (out of scope for MRB v1 — the existing guest flow ends at create, and the desk is the only entity that can guarantee availability + bed-inventory + corporate-cap arithmetic on the fly); rendering the per-child dates in the per-row `Booking` child rows (the child row's "Dates" column already shows the child's own dates — the divergence only matters at the reservation row + drawer + email + receipt level); a separate `divergentDateRanges[]` array (one `actualDateRange` is enough — the children are the source of truth, the header only tracks the MIN/MAX + the flag). **(8) Gates** — MRB-04 (the reservation header + aggregate counters — the new field slots into the existing header shape); MRB-07 (the multi-room `WalkinRoomStay` pattern + per-action scope labels — the new "Add room to reservation" button carries the `renderActionScope("reservation")` chip from MRB-07); MRB-08 (corporate + voucher `usageCount` increment-by-N — the new handler follows the same `+= 1` per room added rule, not `+= assignedRooms.length` for create); MRB-09 (the reservation-scope email view — the new `actualDateRange` field is a property of the view, not a separate parameter); MRB-10 (the reservation-scope guest lookup — the `actualDateRange` field is read by the lookup projection so the guest sees the per-child dates on `/my-booking`); MRB-11 (the stored `revenueAllocation` + the `aggregateRevenueAllocation` — the add-room handler re-stamps the aggregate in the same transaction); MRB-12 (the AdminContext reservations listener + the drawer reservation strip — the same listener the strip's money pills read; the new strip pill is a sibling of the MRB-12 Total/Paid/Balance pills); MRB-13 (the cancel-scope picker routes through the same per-child iteration — the add-room handler reuses the per-child + reservation-scope status matrix); MRB-15 (the report-reconstruction property tests pin the date-divergence accounting — the Java-gated emulator test is the MRB-15 follow-up). **(9) Driving bug** — pre-MRB-14, `handleRescheduleBooking` did `transaction.update(reservationDocRef, { checkIn, checkOut, numNights, ... })` to the new child's dates. When the desk extended one room by 2 nights, the header's "Jan 1 → Jan 5" became the new child's "Jan 1 → Jan 7" — and every other room in the reservation, the email subject, the receipt PDF, the dashboard's date filter, and the checkin reminder cron all suddenly showed "Jan 1 → Jan 7" even though the other 2 rooms were still "Jan 1 → Jan 5." The fix is to **freeze the header's `checkIn` / `checkOut` at create time** (they become the original shared dates) and add a new `actualDateRange` field for the children's spread. Once children diverge, every UI surface + email + receipt renders per-child dates. The dashboard's date filter is a MRB-15 follow-up (the per-room dates are surfaced on the row + drawer; the dashboard's date-range filter still reads the header's shared range by design — a divergent reservation is rare enough that the staff filters the children directly via the row's tooltip + the per-room `Dates` column on the expanded child rows). |
| 181 | **MRB-15 Remaining Tests + MD Sync (shipped 2026-08-03, implementation record):** eight audit sub-items closed out (v0.249.0 → v0.255.0) on `feature/phase-12-mrb-15-01-lifecycle-invariants` through `feature/phase-12-mrb-15-08-legacy-fallback` (8 feature branches + 8 merge commits into `dev`). **Driving theme** — the MRB-01 → MRB-14 work introduced a new reservation-scope layer (the `Reservation` header + per-child `bookings/{id}.reservationId` foreign key + the `kind: "reservation"` email/lookup shapes); the MRB-15 audits guard the cross-cutting invariants the new layer depends on, in source-text form (cheap, deterministic, <5s per file per `plan/docs/CONTRIBUTING.md §Testing`). The behavioural emulator tests (the `firebase/tests/mrb-15-*.emulator.test.ts` round-trips) are deferred to the local environment that has the Java emulator (mirrors the MRB-11 + CRL-09 + MRB-14 precedent). **(1) MRB-15-01 — Lifecycle invariants (v0.249.0, 14 tests in `mrb-15-01-lifecycle-invariants.test.ts`)** — across the full create → cancel lifecycle, exactly ONE `roomCount` increment at create, exactly ONE `activeRoomCount` increment at create, exactly ONE `loyalty` earn entry (`earn-${bookingId}` construction) on the eventual check-out (the cancel-side clawback at MRB-05 already guards the negative side), exactly ONE per-action email template render (no `booking-cancelled` AND `booking-cancelled-reservation` firing for the same destroy), exactly ONE `cancelledRoomCount` increment per cancelled child, exactly ONE `clawback-${bookingId}` pointsHistory entry per cancelled child. The map-based `pointsHistory` invariant `rewardsPoints == sum(pointsHistory.points)` is preserved end-to-end. **(2) MRB-15-02 — Payment-vs-room state (v0.249.0, embedded in 15-01)** — the `paymentStatus` aggregate reads from the child `childStatuses` array (NOT a hardcoded `["checked-in"]` / `["checked-out"]` literal — that was the MRB-15-03 fix below). **(3) MRB-15-03 — Transactional summary counters (v0.250.0, 13 tests in `mrb-15-03-transactional-counters.test.ts` + 4 updated MRB-05 tests)** — **REAL BUG FIX**: pre-MRB-15-03, `handleCheckinBooking` and `handleCheckoutBooking` updated the booking's own `status` but NEVER recomputed the header's `checkedInRoomCount` / `checkedOutRoomCount` — the counters were silently stuck at `0` forever. The fix reads the children via `adminDb.collection("bookings").where("reservationId", "==", bookingReservationId)` in the SAME `runTransaction` and recomputes the aggregate `paymentStatus` from the actual `childStatuses` (not the hardcoded `["checked-in"]` / `["checked-out"]` literal — correct for N>1 mixed states). The `paymentStatus` aggregate is now a true `computeReservationAggregatePaymentStatus(postStatuses)` derivation. Counter ownership contract is newly documented: `roomCount`→create+add-room, `activeRoomCount`→create+add-room+cancel, `cancelledRoomCount`→cancel only, `checkedInRoomCount`→check-in+check-out (read+write), `checkedOutRoomCount`→check-out only. **(4) MRB-15-04 — N=1 + legacy null-`reservationId` byte-equivalence (v0.251.0, 16 tests in `mrb-15-04-single-room-header-path.test.ts`)** — for an N=1 reservation, the handler's output is byte-equivalent to the pre-MRB-01 single-booking path. For a legacy pre-MRB-01 booking (no `reservationId`), the handler's output is byte-equivalent to the pre-MRB-01 single-booking path. The N=1 path carries `kind: "single"` (not `kind: "reservation"`) on the lookup response — falls through to the per-child view. **(5) MRB-15-05 — Canonical/copy consistency (v0.252.0, 16 tests in `mrb-15-05-canonical-copy.test.ts`)** — no real drift found; codebase was already consistent. The audit pins the canonical copy: British "Cancelled" (not American "Canceled"); title-case StatusBadge labels; subject pattern `[${brandName}] <verb>: ${ref} (N room[s])`; preheaders end with period. **(6) MRB-15-06 — PEX (hold-expires) fan-out (v0.253.0, 15 tests in `mrb-15-06-pex-fan-out.test.ts`)** — no real drift found; codebase was already consistent. The audit pins the `holdExpiresAt` fan-out contract: `computeHoldExpiresAt` is the single stamping helper; add-room INHERITS `reservation.holdExpiresAt ?? null` (no fresh stamp); reschedule PRESERVES (Firestore field-level merge); walkin stamps `null`; reject-payment stamps a fresh value. Every `holdExpiresAt`-touching path is covered (PEX-01..06). **(7) MRB-15-07 — Checkout + loyalty earn audit (v0.254.0, 13 tests in `mrb-15-07-checkout-loyalty-earn.test.ts`)** — the loyalty earn path (positive side that pairs with MRB-15-01's clawback). The audit pins: `earn-${bookingId}` paired with `clawback-${bookingId}`; exactly 2 actual constructions of `earn-${bookingId}` (checkout's `awardNow` + post-settlement); exactly 1 `clawback-${bookingId}` construction (in `handleCancelBooking`). The 3rd reference is a JSDoc link, not a construction. **(8) MRB-15-08 — Legacy null-`reservationId` fallback audit (v0.255.0, 19 tests in `mrb-15-08-legacy-fallback.test.ts`)** — every MRB path's null-`reservationId` branch falls through to the legacy single-booking path. `handleCreateBooking` / `handleCreateWalkin` ALWAYS mint a fresh `reservationId` (server auto-mints when the body omits it; client may preallocate for idempotency). Legacy null-`reservationId` bookings can only exist from pre-MRB-01 docs already in the database — the only way to have one in post-MRB-01 code is a pre-existing doc. The audit pins: cancel falls through to per-child (the reservation-scope branch is gated on BOTH `scope === "reservation"` AND `lookedUpReservationId.length > 0`); check-in / check-out / verify-payment / mark-payment-confirmed skip the header mirror (gated on `bookingReservationId.length > 0`); add-payment / add-refund use the dual-source pattern (legacy = `bookings/{id}/payments/`, new = `reservations/{id}/payments/` + `/refunds/`); `readTransactionalFolioSnapshot` early-returns; `loadReservationEmailView` returns `null`; `handleLookupBooking` returns `kind: "single"`; `handleRecordCancellationException` / `handleGetCancellationLiability` use the dual-source read. CRL-07 cancellation liability snapshot lives on the booking doc for legacy (the booking IS the reservation) and on the header for new reservation-scope cancels. **`handleAddRefund` and `handleMarkPaymentConfirmed` do NOT mirror the header** (the mirror is reserved for `handleVerifyAndRecordPayment` which fires from the payment-uploaded transition — the mark-payment-confirmed handler is a thin status flipper; the add-refund handler is a money path that only appends a refund record). **Test coverage** — 14 + 13 + 16 + 16 + 15 + 13 + 19 = **106 source-text tests** across 7 new test files (plus 4 updated MRB-05 tests + 1 MRB-12 rewrite = **111 net new tests** in the MRB-15 umbrella). **Rejected** — full behavioural emulator tests (the `firebase/tests/mrb-15-*.emulator.test.ts` round-trips) at this stage (deferred to the Java-emulator env; the source-text guards are the higher-signal lower-cost coverage); fixing any of the 7 no-drift audits (the audit goal was to pin the existing contract, not to refactor); expanding the MRB-15-08 audit to cover the per-action email `kind` shapes (the email + lookup shapes are MRB-09 + MRB-10 territory, already tested); rewriting the 8 test files to share a common base (each audit has a distinct contract — sharing a base would couple them and obscure the per-audit contract). **Gates** — MRB-01 (the `reservationId` field + the `Reservation` header shape), MRB-04 (the reservation-scope aggregate + the new `actualDateRange`), MRB-05 (the header mirror for payment status + the loyalty earn/clawback), MRB-09 (the reservation-scope email view), MRB-10 (the reservation-scope guest lookup), MRB-11 (the per-child `revenueAllocation` + the `aggregateRevenueAllocation`), MRB-13 (the cancel-scope picker), MRB-14 (the immutable header dates + the add-room endpoint). **Follow-ups (not blocking)** — `firebase/tests/mrb-15-*.emulator.test.ts` round-trips deferred to the Java-emulator env; a future MRB-15.x item may add the per-action `kind` shape to the email + lookup response (the current audit pins the existing per-action split). **Driving bug** — MRB-15-03 was the only audit that found a real bug: pre-MRB-15-03, `handleCheckinBooking` and `handleCheckoutBooking` did `transaction.update(reservationRef, { checkedInRoomCount: ???, checkedOutRoomCount: ??? })` but the source of those values was missing — the counters were never recomputed, so a 2-room reservation that fully checked in still showed `checkedInRoomCount: 0` on the header. The aggregate `paymentStatus` was a hardcoded `["checked-in"]` / `["checked-out"]` array literal that was only correct for N=1. |
