Clicks `+` again → 5 guests + 4 children (doesn't fit, `+` button is now disabled at the room's cap of 3). The CHD-11 capacity chip on the room card updates live (Fits / Tight / Doesn't fit). The "at least 1 adult" invariant is still preserved via auto-bump. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `guests` state stays, `numChildren` state stays, `numAdults = max(0, guests - numChildren)` derivation stays. The `setOccupancy` helper is new (extracted from `updateGuests` + `updateChildren`). The `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` derivation is updated (same name, new formula). **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (~20 lines changed: extracted helper + refactored functions + updated derivation + updated disabled condition). **Tests** — new `guest-app/tests/api/chd-11-1-picker-auto-bump-guests.test.ts` (~8 source-text guards: the `setOccupancy` helper is defined; `updateGuests` calls it; `updateChildren` does the auto-bump; the `guests - 1` clamp is gone from `updateChildren`; the new derivation uses `effectiveGuests = max(guests, N + 1)`; the `+` button's disabled condition uses `selectedMaxSelectableChildren`). **MDs to update** — `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (this entry, ⬜ open), `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #192` (this decision), `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §CHD-11.1 — Picker Auto-Bumps Guests to Fit More Children` (the spec above). **Rejected** — don't auto-bump, let user manually bump guests first (2-step action, worse UX); show a hint with a quick action (more UI than auto-bump); remove the "at least 1 adult" invariant entirely (violates CHD-05); keep the existing derivation bounded by `guests - 1` (cap would be wrong for higher children counts); show a modal asking "Bump up to 3 guests?" (modal is friction). **Gates** — CHD-05 (server still validates `numAdults + numChildren === numGuests` and `numAdults >= 1`; the client-side auto-bump maintains the invariant), CHD-11 (the per-type `maxChildren` cap is still the upper bound; the picker just allows the user to explore up to that cap; the capacity chip is the verification surface), EXB-11 + EXB-11.1 + EXB-11.2 (the extras toggle uses the same `requiredExtraBedsFor` helper as the new derivation; the auto-init useEffect re-fires when `numAdults` or `numChildren` change), Step 2/3 aside (extra-bed pricing updates live; no change), MRB-15-10 + MRB-15-11 (admin CRUD + photo gallery, unrelated), CHD-13 (homepage search widget, unrelated; the URL pre-fill is unchanged), EXB-11.3 (no default room-type selection, independent; affects room-type card selection, not children stepper). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in CHD-11.1)** — a separate "Adults" stepper on the homepage widget and `/book` picker (instead of the single "Guests" stepper that auto-derives adults); smarter auto-bump with a "room doesn't fit" hint; a "Reset to 1 adult + 0 children" quick action; visual feedback when the auto-bump fires.

| 194 | **CHD-11.3 Symmetric Auto-Bump + Remove All Per-Type Caps from the Picker (proposed 2026-08-05; shipped 2026-08-05 on `feature/chd-11-3-symmetric-auto-bump`; v0.261.0 → v0.262.0):** the children stepper on `/book` Step 1 drops the per-type cap (`selectedMaxSelectableChildren`) from `setOccupancy` and `updateChildren`, and adds a symmetric auto-bump to `updateGuests` so the picker is a true exploration surface with no client-side guards (the "Fits your group" chip + the submit gate do the validation). The pre-CHD-11.3 surface had two residual UX issues that violated the CHD-11 "exploration-first, validation-on-commit" promise. **Driving motivation** — operator-reported UX feedback 2026-08-05 (post-CHD-11.2 review): "the maximum I can have is still 3 — the max config I can have is 4 adults and 3 children, when I make it 6 guests, no more children it becomes 0. Can we not have any guards to the numbers?" **Issue 1: "the maximum I can have is still 3"** — the per-type cap (`selectedMaxSelectableChildren`) was still firing in `setOccupancy` and `updateChildren`. Even with CHD-11.2 (which removed the per-type cap from the `+` button's `disabled` condition), the value silently snapped back to `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` when the user clicked `+` past the cap. **Issue 2: "when I make it 6 guests, no more children it becomes 0"** — the `Math.max(0, safeGuests - 1)` clamp in `setOccupancy` fires when the user lowers `guests` to a value where `children + 1 > guests`. With guests=2 and children=3, the formula says `max(0, 2-1) = 1`, so children drops from 3 to 1. The user was at 3, then adjusted guests down past the "at least 1 adult" floor, and the system silently dropped their children count instead of bumping guests up. **Root cause** — both issues come from the same root: the client-side guards in `setOccupancy` + `updateChildren` are enforcing things the submit gate can validate. Per CHD-11's "exploration-first, validation-on-commit" promise, the picker should be a pure exploration surface. **Fix — three-part change to `BookingPage.tsx`.** **(1) Remove the per-type cap (`selectedMaxSelectableChildren`) from `setOccupancy` + `updateChildren`.** The `safeChildren` clamp chain in `setOccupancy` becomes `Math.max(0, Math.min(nextChildren, safeGuests - 1))` (the "at least 1 adult" invariant only). The `updateChildren`'s `desiredChildren` becomes `Math.max(nextChildren, 0)` (no per-type cap). The `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` derivation stays (used by the chip's hint text), but it's no longer a clamp. **(2) Add a symmetric auto-bump to `updateGuests`.** If the user lowers `guests` to a value where `children + 1 > guests`, auto-bump `guests` up to `children + 1` instead of clamping `children` down. The new `updateGuests` computes `newGuests = Math.max(nextGuests, numChildren + 1)` and calls `setOccupancy(newGuests, numChildren)`. The "at least 1 adult" invariant is maintained via auto-bump, not clamp. **(3) Keep the `Math.max(0, safeGuests - 1)` clamp in `setOccupancy` as defense in depth.** The server-side CHD-05 validation is the authoritative gate, but the client-side mirror is a UX safety net (deep-links, race conditions, edge cases). **Why the symmetric auto-bump is the right shape** — the pre-CHD-11.3 model was asymmetric (only `updateChildren` auto-bumped, `updateGuests` clamped). The asymmetry is unintuitive. The user expects "I pick a number, the system figures out the right total." The fix is to make `updateGuests` symmetric: both functions bump `guests` up; neither clamps `children` down. **What the user sees after the change** — picker is now a pure exploration surface. Click `+` to 4 children with guests=2 → auto-bumps to guests=5, children=4. The "Fits your group" chip says "Doesn't fit" (depending on the room's capacity). The submit gate blocks Step 2. Set guests=1 with children=3 → auto-bumps guests to 4, children=3 (preserved). No more silent snap-backs. No more "children becomes 0" surprises. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `guests` state stays, `numChildren` state stays, `numAdults = max(0, guests - numChildren)` derivation stays, `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` derivation stays (used by the chip's hint text), `setOccupancy` helper stays, `updateChildren` function stays, `updateGuests` function stays. The clamping chain in `setOccupancy` is updated (per-type cap removed). **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (~10 lines changed: 3 lines in `setOccupancy`, 3 lines in `updateChildren`, 3 lines in `updateGuests`, plus comment blocks). **Tests** — new `guest-app/tests/api/chd-11-3-symmetric-auto-bump.test.ts` (~11 source-text guards: the `setOccupancy` clamp chain has no `selectedMaxSelectableChildren`; the `updateChildren` `desiredChildren` has no `selectedMaxSelectableChildren`; the `updateGuests` has the symmetric auto-bump; the `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` derivation is still present; the `+` button's `disabled` condition is `numChildren >= 10`; etc.). Update 2 existing tests in `chd-11-1-picker-auto-bump-guests.test.ts` and `chd-05-guest-child-cap.test.ts` to remove the `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` references. **MDs to update** — `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (this entry, ✅ shipped 2026-08-05), `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #194` (this decision), `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §CHD-11.3 — Symmetric Auto-Bump + Remove All Per-Type Caps from the Picker` (the spec above). **Rejected** — keep the per-type cap in `setOccupancy` but raise it to a higher value (per-type cap is a soft constraint, not a domain rule; the chip + submit gate catch the over-cap case); remove the per-type cap but keep the asymmetric auto-bump (asymmetry is unintuitive); remove the `Math.max(0, safeGuests - 1)` clamp entirely (auto-bump covers the user-driven case, but deep-links and race conditions could bypass it); show a warning when the user picks more children than the room supports (chip + submit gate are the existing surfaces); auto-add a second room when the user is over the cap (presumptuous; chip + submit gate let the user decide). **Gates** — CHD-05 (server still validates `numAdults + numChildren === numGuests` and `numAdults >= 1`; the client-side auto-bump + clamp mirror this; the server is the authoritative gate), CHD-11 (the picker is now a true exploration surface; the "Fits your group" chip + the submit gate are the commit surfaces; the CHD-11 promise is now fully realized), CHD-11.1 (the auto-bump in `updateChildren` is unchanged; CHD-11.3 adds a symmetric auto-bump in `updateGuests`), CHD-11.2 (the picker cap is unchanged; the per-type cap is now gone from the picker), CHD-12 (unchanged), EXB-11 + EXB-11.1 + EXB-11.2 (unchanged), Step 2/3 aside (unchanged), MRB-15-10 + MRB-15-11 (unrelated), CHD-13 (unchanged), EXB-11.3 (independent). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in CHD-11.3)** — per-room-type picker cap; a "Room full" warning separate from the chip; a "Reset to 1 adult + 0 children" quick action; visual feedback when the auto-bump fires; server-side validation for the per-type cap; per-type cap on the homepage widget.

| 193 | **CHD-11.2 Picker Cap Raised to Soft 10 (No Per-Type Cap on the + Button) (proposed 2026-08-05; shipped 2026-08-05 on `feature/chd-11-2-picker-cap-soft-10`; v0.260.1 → v0.261.0):** the children stepper on `/book` Step 1 raises the `+` button's cap from the room's capacity (per CHD-11.1) to the soft 10 (per CHD-11's sanity guard), so the user can pick any number of children and let the "Fits your group" chip + the submit gate do the validation. The pre-CHD-11.2 surface hard-capped the picker at the room's capacity, which is the wrong enforcement surface — per CHD-11's "exploration-first, validation-on-commit" pattern, the picker should be an exploration surface, and the chip + submit gate are the commit surfaces. **Driving motivation** — operator-reported UX feedback 2026-08-05 (post-CHD-11.1 review): "why is it the children + chip is disabled after 3? maybe we can disable that disabling?" Reproduction: 1 Single Room (`maxCapacity: 1`, `maxChildren: 2`, `maxExtraBeds: 1`), 2 guests. The user wants to pick 4 children to see the "Doesn't fit" chip + decide whether to add a second room. The picker blocks them at 3 (the room's capacity). The "Fits your group" chip would have shown "Doesn't fit" and the submit gate would have surfaced the over-cap error, but the picker never gave them the chance to explore. **Root cause** — the pre-CHD-11.2 `+` button's `disabled` condition was `numChildren >= Math.min(10, selectedMaxSelectableChildren)` (per `BookingPage.tsx:2217`). The `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` is the room's capacity (with auto-bump, per CHD-11.1) — for a Single Room, that's 3 children (1 adult + 3 children = 4 occupants, with 1 extra bed, within the hard capacity of `maxCapacity + maxChildren + maxExtraBeds = 1 + 2 + 1 = 4`). The picker is hard-capping at the room's capacity, which is the wrong layer. **Fix — one-line change to `BookingPage.tsx:2217`.** Change the `+` button's `disabled` condition from `numChildren >= Math.min(10, selectedMaxSelectableChildren)` to `numChildren >= 10` (the CHD-11 soft cap, not the room's capacity). The `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` derivation stays (still used by the "Fits your group" chip + the CHD-11 capacity indicator at `BookingPage.tsx:2175` and the cart summary). The `−` button's `disabled` condition (`numChildren <= 0`) is unchanged. The `updateChildren` function (with the CHD-11.1 auto-bump) is unchanged. The URL pre-fill from `searchParams.get("children")` is unchanged. **What the user sees after the change** — 1 Single Room, 2 guests, clicks `+` 3 times → 3 children, 3 guests (auto-bump), `+` still enabled. "Fits your group" chip shows. Clicks `+` once more → 4 children, 4 guests (auto-bump), `+` still enabled. "Doesn't fit" chip shows (the room needs 3 extra beds, maxExtraBeds 1). Clicks `+` repeatedly until 10 children, 10 guests, `+` disabled at the soft cap. The user can now explore freely: pick any number, the chip + submit gate tell them if it works. They can decide whether to add a second room, remove children, or pick a different room type. **Why the soft 10 is the right cap (not the room's capacity, not unlimited)** — the CHD-11 spec's `10` is a UX sanity guard (no one books a 50-child hotel room via the homepage widget). The pre-CHD-11.2 cap of `MIN(10, selectedMaxSelectableChildren)` overrode the 10 with the room's capacity, which is the wrong layer. The new cap `10` is higher than the per-type capacity for most room types (a Family Room's `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` is typically 4-6, so the soft 10 is more permissive), lower than "unlimited" (a sanity guard against pathological inputs), and consistent with the homepage widget's children picker (CHD-13), which also uses 0-10. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `numChildren` state stays. `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` derivation stays (used by the chip + capacity indicator). `updateChildren` function is unchanged. `setOccupancy` helper is unchanged. The auto-bump is unchanged. The submit gate is unchanged. **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (~5 lines changed: 1 regex update + the comment block). **Tests** — new `guest-app/tests/api/chd-11-2-picker-cap-soft-10.test.ts` (~7 source-text guards: the `+` button's disabled condition is `numChildren >= 10`; the soft 10 is the right balance); cap at `MIN(20, selectedMaxSelectableChildren)` or some other higher value (the CHD-11 spec's 10 is the right sanity guard); cap at `guests` (the pre-CHD-11.1 behavior; the auto-bump in CHD-11.1 already handles the invariant; the soft 10 is the only cap needed); remove the cap AND remove the auto-bump (violates the CHD-05 contract); show a "Room full" tooltip on the `+` button (a tooltip is a friction point; the "Doesn't fit" chip + the submit gate are the existing surfaces). **Gates** — CHD-05 (server still validates `numAdults + numChildren === numGuests` and `numAdults >= 1`; the client-side picker is just less restrictive; the server is the authoritative gate), CHD-11 (the picker is now a true exploration surface; the "Fits your group" chip + the submit gate are the commit surfaces; the CHD-11 promise is now fully realized), CHD-11.1 (the auto-bump in `updateChildren` is unchanged; the new cap is consistent), EXB-11.3 (no default room-type selection, independent). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in CHD-11.2)** — a more aggressive "Fits your group" chip when the user is over the room's capacity; a "Room full" tooltip on the `+` button; a "0 adults + N children" mode for dorm-style rooms; a "Reset to 1 adult + 0 children" quick action; per-room-type picker cap.

| 195 | **CHD-11.4 Free Expression in the Picker (Submit-Gate Enforces "At Least 1 Adult") (proposed 2026-08-05; shipped 2026-08-05 on `feature/chd-11-4-picker-free-expression`; v0.262.0 → v0.263.0):** the children stepper on `/book` Step 1 drops the "at least 1 adult" auto-bump from `updateChildren` and `updateGuests`, and drops the `Math.max(0, safeGuests - 1)` clamp from `setOccupancy`. The submit gate catches the 0-adults case (the `numAdults >= 1` check at `BookingPage.tsx:724` + the per-room check at `cartDistributionComplete`). **What the user sees after the change** — picker is a free expression surface. Set Guests=5, Children=5 → picker shows 5 / 5 / 0 adults (derived). The Continue button is disabled with "Choose enough rooms to assign every adult and child, with at least one adult in each room." | 196 | **CHD-11.5 Explicit Adults + Children Picker (Replace Guests Stepper with Adults Stepper) (proposed 2026-08-05; shipped 2026-08-05 on `feature/chd-11-5-explicit-adults-picker`; v0.263.0 → v0.264.0):** the /book Step 1 picker drops the "Guests" (total) stepper and adds an "Adults" stepper; the Children stepper stays. URL contract changes from `?guests=N&children=N` to `?adults=N&children=N` (backward compat: `?guests=N&children=N` is still accepted and converted). **Driving motivation** — operator-reported UX feedback 2026-08-05 (post-CHD-11.4 review, with screenshot showing 17 guests, 10 children): "I was thinking of instead of asking the number of guests, lets just ask the number of adults and children." The pre-CHD-11.5 surface asks for the total + a sub-count, with `numAdults` derived as `guests - numChildren`. The user has to do mental math: "I have 4 adults and 3 kids" → "7 guests + 3 children." The new shape removes the derivation. **Why this is the right shape** — (1) more direct (the user picks the domain model directly), (2) the "at least 1 adult" rule is enforced at the Adults stepper's min (1), not via the submit gate (the submit gate becomes defense in depth), (3) the 0-adults case is naturally impossible (the picker can't produce an invalid state via the UI), (4) matches the homepage widget (CHD-13) which already has separate Adults + Children steppers, (5) CHD-11.1 + CHD-11.3 + CHD-11.4 are all moot — the picker becomes a "naturally" free expression surface because the user picks adults directly. **Fix — multi-part change to `BookingPage.tsx` + `HomePage.tsx`.** **(1) Replace the "Guests" state with "Adults" state.** `const [guests, setGuests] = useState(...)` becomes `const [adults, setAdults] = useState(...)`. The `numAdults` derivation `Math.max(0, guests - numChildren)` is gone — `numAdults = adults` (the state). The `guests` value (for the server + cart summary) is derived as `const guests = adults + numChildren`. **(2) Rename `updateGuests` → `updateAdults` and `setOccupancy(nextGuests, nextChildren)` → `setOccupancy(nextAdults, nextChildren)`.** The `setOccupancy` body clamps `safeAdults = Math.min(Math.max(nextAdults, 1), maxGuestCapacity)` + `safeChildren = Math.max(0, Math.min(nextChildren, safeAdults))`. The URL write changes `next.set("guests", ...)` to `next.set("adults", ...)`. **(3) Update the URL contract: `?guests=N&children=N` → `?adults=N&children=N`.** New canonical shape. Backward compat: the URL reader at line 214 reads `searchParams.get("adults")` first, falls back to `searchParams.get("guests")` (with `adults = max(1, guests - children)`). **(4) Replace the "Guests" stepper with the "Adults" stepper in the picker UI.** The Adults stepper has min 1, max `maxGuestCapacity`. The Children stepper stays. The helper text under Children is updated to reference `adults` instead of `guests`. **(5) Update `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` derivation.** The historical auto-bump scenario is gone — `effectiveGuests = adults + children` is just the total. **(6) Update `HomePage` URL writing.** `searchAvailability` writes `?adults=N&children=N` (no `guests` param). **(7) Update the submit gate's "at least 1 adult" check.** The existing `numAdults >= 1` check at line 724 is now redundant but stays as defense in depth. The per-room `cartDistributionComplete` check at line 463 is unchanged. **What the user sees after the change** — picker has two steppers: "Adults" (1-N) and "Children (0-11)" (0-10). The user's screenshot case (17 guests, 10 children, 7 adults) maps to Adults=7, Children=10. Deep link to `?guests=5&children=2` derives `adults = 3`; the picker shows Adults=3, Children=2. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `numAdults` state is now the state (was derived). `numChildren` state stays. The server-side `numAdults + numChildren === guests` consistency check is preserved. The cart serialization is unchanged. **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (~30 lines: state rename + `setOccupancy` rename + picker UI + derivation + URL reader/writer) + `guest-app/src/pages/HomePage.tsx` (~3 lines: URL writing). **Tests** — new `guest-app/tests/api/chd-11-5-explicit-adults-picker.test.ts` (~13 source-text guards). Update 5 prior tests (`chd-05-guest-child-cap.test.ts`, `chd-11-1-picker-auto-bump-guests.test.ts`, `chd-11-2-picker-cap-soft-10.test.ts`, `chd-11-3-symmetric-auto-bump.test.ts`, `chd-11-4-picker-free-expression.test.ts`) to reflect the new state shape. **MDs to update** — `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (this entry, ✅ shipped 2026-08-05), `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #196` (this decision), `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §CHD-11.5 — Explicit Adults + Children Picker` (the spec above). **Rejected** — keep the "Guests + Children" shape but rename "Guests" to "Adults" (semantically wrong; "Guests" means the total, "Adults" means a sub-count); add a separate "Adults" stepper alongside the "Guests" stepper (overengineered; the Adults picker REPLACES the Guests picker); change the URL contract without backward compat (breaks deep links from CHD-13 + SEO + external links); server-side change to drop the `guests` param (no change needed; the server already takes `numAdults + numChildren`). **Gates** — CHD-05 (server contract unchanged; the client's new shape sends the same data, just `numAdults` is now the state), CHD-11 (the picker is now a true exploration surface; the chip + submit gate are the only validation surfaces), CHD-11.1 (auto-bump reversed by CHD-11.4, moot in CHD-11.5), CHD-11.2 (soft cap of 10 stays), CHD-11.3 (auto-bump reversed by CHD-11.4, moot in CHD-11.5; per-type cap removal stays), CHD-11.4 (the "submit gate catches 0 adults" check is now redundant; stays as defense in depth), CHD-12 (cart summary unchanged), CHD-13 (homepage widget state unchanged; URL writing updated to write `?adults=N&children=N`), EXB-11 + EXB-11.1 + EXB-11.2 (extras toggle re-fires when `numAdults` or `numChildren` change), Step 2/3 aside (unchanged), MRB-15-10 + MRB-15-11 (unrelated), EXB-11.3 (independent). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in CHD-11.5)** — rename the homepage widget's "Guests" trigger button to "People" or "Travelers"; add a "Reset to 1 adult + 0 children" quick action; show the total `guests = adults + children` in the picker UI; server-side allow `numAdults: 0` per-room (relax CHD-05); a "0 adults + N children" preset button (out of scope; the new picker enforces min 1 at the Adults stepper).

| 197 | **EXB-11.4 Revert Auto-Initiate Extra Bed Count; User Is in Full Control (proposed 2026-08-06; shipped 2026-08-06 on `fix/exb-11-4-no-auto-init-extra-bed`; v0.264.2 → v0.264.3):** the EXB-11.2 auto-init `useEffect` at `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx:778-808` is removed, the `displayExtraBeds` derivation in the Extras IIFE is dropped, the checkbox + counter `[−]` disabled conditions are relaxed to just `typeQuantity === 0` (the user can tick/untick freely and can go below the soft floor), and the `aria-describedby` is aligned with the warning's actual render condition (`overCap` instead of `softFloor >= 1`). The cart starts at 0 and stays at 0 until the user explicitly ticks the checkbox or increments the counter. The soft floor is still computed for the over-cap warning text + the submit gate's `cartFitsGroup` check, but no longer feeds the visual or the cart. **Driving motivation** — operator-reported UX bug 2026-08-06 (post-EXB-11.2 review): "in the /book page, I am unable to tick the extra bed? it is ticked by default? please do not make choices for the user, let the user tick or untick." Two symptoms of the same EXB-11.2 design choice: **(1) the extra bed is ticked by default** — the auto-init `useEffect` silently bumps the cart's per-type `extraBedCount` to the soft floor (minimum required for the group to fit), so the extra-bed checkbox renders as `checked` even on first render and the counter starts at the soft-floor value instead of 0; **(2) the user can't change it** — the checkbox is also `disabled` (per EXB-11.2's `|| softFloor >= 1` rule), so the user can't uncheck it; the counter's `[−]` is also disabled at the soft floor, so the user can't go below. The user is stuck in a state they didn't choose. **Root cause** — the EXB-11.2 design chose the wrong enforcement surface for the soft floor. The soft floor is a *constraint* (the group needs at least N extra beds to fit), but the EXB-11.2 surface turned it into a *choice* (the system silently sets the count to N). Three specific design choices from EXB-11.2 that violated the EXB-11 "user is in control of the cart, not the system" promise: **(a) the auto-init `useEffect`** — silently mutated the cart on every render where `softFloor > userExtraBeds`, without any user input; **(b) the `displayExtraBeds` derivation** — decoupled the visual from the cart (the visual showed the soft floor, the cart was bumped to match), creating a derived state that hid the user's actual choice; **(c) the `disabled = ... || softFloor >= 1` rule on the checkbox + the `disabled = ... || userExtraBeds <= softFloor` rule on the counter `[−]`** — enforced the soft floor at the UI layer, blocking the user from reaching a valid state they wanted to be in. The user is the one paying for the extra bed; the user should be the one choosing it. The EXB-11 spec's promise was right; EXB-11.2 reversed it; EXB-11.4 restores it. **Why this is the right shape** — **(1) honest UI** — the checkbox's `checked` reads from the cart, the counter's display reads from the cart, the stay total reads from the cart. No derived state, no off-by-one between the visual and the price. The user sees exactly what they're going to pay for. **(2) user agency** — the user can tick/untick freely, increment/decrement freely, go to 0 even if the group needs the bed. The system doesn't make the choice; the user does. **(3) submit gate as the validation surface** — the per CHD-11 "exploration-first, validation-on-commit" pattern, the under-floor case is caught at Step 1 → Step 2 by the `cartFitsGroup` check (the Continue button is disabled with the "Adjust room" CTA). The user who unticks the extra bed and tries to continue sees the gate fire with a clear next-step. **(4) over-cap warning still fires** — when `softFloor > typeMaxExtraBeds` (the group physically can't fit in the type's bed capacity), the soft-floor warning text renders below the toggle ("Room needs N extra beds to fit your group. You can add up to M here."). This is the case where the user's free choice is bounded by a real physical constraint, not a UI policy. **(5) a11y fix bundled in** — the pre-EXB-11.4 checkbox `aria-describedby` was wired to `softFloor >= 1` (the soft-floor condition), but the warning text only renders on `overCap` (soft floor > cap). The `aria-describedby` now points to the warning only when the warning is on screen (`overCap`), so screen readers don't announce a non-existent element. This is a pre-existing bug from EXB-11.2 that EXB-11.4 fixes as part of the visual revert. **What the user sees now** — **(1) Single Room (`maxExtraBeds: 1`), 2 guests** — soft floor 1, cap 1, not over-cap. Cart starts at 0. Checkbox renders `unchecked + enabled` (was `checked + disabled`). The user can tick the box to add the bed (cart = 1), or leave it unticked and hit Continue → "Adjust room" CTA. The soft-floor warning does NOT fire (soft floor = 1, not over-cap; the cap matches the floor). **(2) Family Room (`maxExtraBeds: 2`), 5 adults** — soft floor 1, cap 2, not over-cap. Cart starts at 0. Counter shows 0 (was 1). Both buttons enabled (the `[−]` was disabled at the soft floor pre-EXB-11.4; now enabled). The user can go from 0 → 1 → 2 freely. **(3) 3 adults in a Single Room (`maxExtraBeds: 1`)** — soft floor 2, cap 1, `overCap = true`. Cart starts at 0. Checkbox renders `unchecked + enabled`. The soft-floor warning fires: "Single Room needs 2 extra beds to fit your group. You can add up to 1 here." The user can tick the box (cart = 1) but the submit gate still blocks them (the group needs 2 beds, the cap is 1). The "Adjust room" CTA guides them to add a second room. **(4) Family Room (`maxExtraBeds: 2`), 4 adults** — soft floor 0, cap 2, not over-cap. Cart starts at 0. Counter shows 0, both buttons enabled. No auto-init, no warning. The user adds a bed if they want; the system doesn't push them. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `room.extraBedCount: number` stays. `updateExtraBedCount` helper is unchanged (still clamps to `[0, typeMaxExtraBeds]`). Cart URL serialization (`rooms=`) is unchanged. Per-type cart summary pill is unchanged (reads from the cart, which now starts at 0; the pill shows "0 extra beds" for the under-floor case, which is the honest signal). Step 2/3 aside aggregation is unchanged (`totalExtraBeds` still sums the per-room `extraBedCount`). Server-side validation is unchanged (the server still validates against `maxExtraBeds` and snapshots `extraBedRate`). The over-cap soft-floor warning text is unchanged. The submit gate's `cartFitsGroup` check is unchanged. **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (the auto-init `useEffect` at lines 754-809 is removed; the `displayExtraBeds = Math.max(softFloor, userExtraBeds)` derivation in the Extras IIFE is removed; the checkbox `disabled` reverts from `typeQuantity === 0 || softFloor >= 1` to just `typeQuantity === 0`; the counter `[−]` `disabled` reverts from `typeQuantity === 0 || userExtraBeds <= softFloor` (or `displayExtraBeds <= softFloor` in EXB-11.2) to just `typeQuantity === 0`; the counter `[+]` `disabled` reverts from `typeQuantity === 0 || displayExtraBeds >= typeMaxExtraBeds` to `typeQuantity === 0 || userExtraBeds >= typeMaxExtraBeds`; the checkbox `checked` reverts from `displayExtraBeds === 1` to `userExtraBeds === 1`; the counter display reverts from `displayExtraBeds` to `userExtraBeds`; the stay total reverts from `displayExtraBeds * typeExtraBedRate * nights` to `userExtraBeds * typeExtraBedRate * nights`; the stay-total render gate reverts from `displayExtraBeds > 0` to `userExtraBeds > 0`; the counter onClick handlers revert from `displayExtraBeds ± 1` to `userExtraBeds ± 1`; the checkbox `aria-describedby` reverts from `softFloor >= 1 ? ... : undefined` to `overCap ? ... : undefined`; the IIFE comment block is updated to document the new behavior). ~80 lines changed. The `updateExtraBedCount` helper at `BookingPage.tsx:957` is unchanged. The `requiredExtraBedsFor` helper (used to derive the soft floor) is unchanged. The submit gate's `cartFitsGroup` check is unchanged. **Tests** — extend `guest-app/tests/api/exb-11-user-controlled-extra-bed-toggle.test.ts` (the existing EXB-11 + EXB-11.1 source-text tests are updated to use `userExtraBeds` instead of `displayExtraBeds` — the counter's `[+]` disabled condition, the counter's `[−]` disabled condition (now just `typeQuantity === 0`), the checkbox `checked` (now `userExtraBeds === 1`), the checkbox `disabled` (now just `typeQuantity === 0`), the `aria-describedby` (now `overCap ? ... : undefined`), the `updateExtraBedCount` onClick handlers (now `userExtraBeds + 1`); the EXB-11.2 describe block is removed; a new EXB-11.4 describe block adds 6 source-text guards: the IIFE does NOT define `displayExtraBeds`; the stay total reads from `userExtraBeds`; the stay-total gate is `userExtraBeds > 0`; there is NO auto-init useEffect; the IIFE comment block documents the EXB-11.4 reversal; the IIFE comment block explicitly notes the user-explicit "no auto-init" stance). The behavioural emulator test (the user ticks/unticks the checkbox on a real room type, sees the per-night price + stay total update; the user increments/decrements the counter below the soft floor; the user can submit with 0 extra beds and sees the "Adjust room" CTA) is out of scope for this sandbox. **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §EXB-11.4 — Revert Auto-Initiate Extra Bed Count; User Is in Full Control`, this decision entry, `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (this entry, ✅ shipped 2026-08-06). **Rejected** — keep the auto-init, just un-disable the checkbox (the user explicitly said "do not make choices for the user" — the auto-init IS the choice; removing the disable doesn't fix the root cause; the user would still see a checked checkbox they didn't choose, and the counter would still start at the soft floor); keep the auto-init but only enforce it on the visual (not the cart — i.e., the `displayExtraBeds` derivation stays, the cart stays at 0) (same root cause: the visual says "checked" but the cart says 0; the per-type cart summary pill in CHD-12 would show "0 extra beds" when the system requires 1, creating a display/cart mismatch the user can see; the price breakdown at Step 3 would show "0 extra beds" while the visual at Step 1 says "checked"); keep the `[−]` disabled at the soft floor (the user explicitly said "do not make choices for the user" — the `[−]` enforcement IS a choice; the submit gate is the right surface for the under-floor case; the user who wants 0 extra beds can go to 0, hit Continue, and see the "Adjust room" CTA — a clear, honest path); keep the `aria-describedby` at `softFloor >= 1` (the pre-EXB-11.4 condition points to a non-existent element in the common case — soft floor = 1, not over-cap; the fix aligns the `aria-describedby` with the warning's actual render condition so screen readers don't announce a non-existent element); show a "we added an extra bed for your group" toast notification on auto-init (a notification is patronizing; EXB-11.4 removes the auto-init entirely, so the toast is moot); add a "we recommend N extra beds" hint next to the checkbox (a hint is a soft push, not a choice — but the user wants no system guidance at all; the over-cap warning text stays for the case where the group physically can't fit, and the submit gate catches the under-floor case; a recommendation hint is a softer version of the same anti-pattern); revert the soft-floor floor on the `[−]` only (keep the checkbox relaxed) (inconsistent UX — the user would have full control on the checkbox case (`maxExtraBeds === 1`) but constrained control on the counter case (`maxExtraBeds >= 2`); the user explicitly asked for full control on both); auto-init as a hidden `?autoInitExtraBeds=true` URL param for A/B testing (a future testing work item; out of scope — the operator's ask is "no auto-init", and a hidden URL param is a different shape). **Gates** — EXB-11 (the underlying contract is unchanged; the toggle is still per-type, the soft floor is still derived from `requiredExtraBedsFor`, the per-type mirror via `updateExtraBedCount` is unchanged), EXB-11.1 (the placement + checkbox-for-`maxExtraBeds === 1` shape is unchanged; only the auto-init is removed), EXB-11.2 (the auto-init `useEffect` + `displayExtraBeds` derivation are removed; the soft-floor `<= cap` infinite-loop guard added in the 2026-08-06 hotfix is moot), CHD-11 (the capacity chip is at the top of the card and is unchanged; the Extras move doesn't touch the chip), CHD-12 (the per-type cart summary's "N extra beds" inline pill is unchanged — it reads from the cart, which now starts at 0; the pill shows "0 extra beds" for the under-floor case, which is the honest signal), EXB-01..10 (server-side contract is unchanged; the cart writes the same per-room `extraBedCount`; the server still validates against `maxExtraBeds` and snapshots `extraBedRate`), Step 2/3 aside (unchanged — reads from the cart's `totalExtraBeds`), MRB-15-10 (the admin surface for editing `maxExtraBeds` and `extraBedRate` is the input side; EXB-11.4 only changes the *client-side selection surface*), MRB-15-11 (unrelated; photo gallery, not extras toggle), CHD-13 (unrelated; homepage search widget). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in EXB-11.4)** — auto-init as a hidden `?autoInitExtraBeds=true` URL param for A/B testing (a future testing work item; out of scope — the operator's ask is "no auto-init", and a hidden URL param is a different shape); a "Recommended: N extra beds" badge on the card (a label, not a fix; the user wants no system guidance); a "We added this for you" toast on auto-init (moot — auto-init is removed); per-room individual extra-bed toggles (let the user add an extra bed to one Single but not the other; carried over from EXB-11's Phase 2 list); a "Reset to 0 extra beds" quick action (out of scope — the counter's `[−]` is now freely clickable, the user can reset to 0 manually).

| 198 | **EXB-11.5 Rate Option Toggle on /book Step 1 (User Can Untick Room Only / Room + Breakfast) (proposed 2026-08-06; shipped 2026-08-06 on `fix/exb-11-5-rate-option-toggle`; v0.264.3 → v0.264.4):** the `selectRoomType` function at `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx:989` is refactored from a one-way add/update to a per-type toggle. Click a rate option on a card → 1 room of that type is added to the cart (or the rate is updated in place). Click the same rate option again → all rooms of that type are removed from the cart (per-type toggle, not per-room). Click a different rate option → the rate is updated in place for every room of that type. The `selectedRoomType` / `rateChoice` state sync is skipped on the untick path — the `useEffect` at `BookingPage.tsx:865` picks another type (or clears) when the current selection is no longer in the cart. The hint text on `/book` Step 1 updates from "Select Room Only or Room + Breakfast to lock the Step 1 summary" to "Click a rate to add a room. Click the same rate again to remove it." — a two-way instruction that teaches the toggle. **Driving motivation** — operator-reported UX feedback 2026-08-06 (post-EXB-11.4 review): "can we allow user to untick the room only and room + breakfast option?" The pre-EXB-11.5 surface (post-EXB-11.3) had a one-way rate-option click: clicking a rate option added the room (or updated the rate if the type was already in the cart), but clicking the same rate again was a no-op — the user had no way to "untick" the room via the rate option itself. The only way to remove a room was via the "Rooms" stepper (decrement to 0), which is a per-quantity control, not a per-type toggle. The rate option is a per-type control (one toggle per type, not per room); it should be toggleable. Same "user is in full control" pattern as EXB-11.4 (extra-bed toggle), different surface. **Root cause** — the pre-EXB-11.5 `selectRoomType` function had a single ternary: `hasType ? updateRate : addRoom`. The "type in cart" branch only updated the rate; it never removed the type. The user could add a room and switch between Room Only / Room + Breakfast, but they couldn't deselect the room via the rate option. The `RateOption` component is a `<button>` (per `BookingPage.tsx:3425`), and the `onSelect` handler always adds or updates — never removes. **Three reasons this was the wrong shape** — (1) **inconsistent with the per-type toggle pattern** — the rate option is a per-type control (one button per type, not per room), and the natural mental model is "click to add, click again to remove" (the same pattern as the extra-bed checkbox added in EXB-11.4). The "Rooms" stepper is the per-quantity control; the rate option is the per-type control. (2) **hidden state mutation** — the user adds a room via the rate option, then has to find the "Rooms" stepper to remove it. The rate option's "active" state (the checkmark) shows the room is in the cart, but the only way to change that state is via a different control. (3) **asymmetric with the extra-bed toggle** — the extra-bed checkbox (EXB-11.4) is a toggle (tick/untick), the breakfast-includes-children toggle is a toggle, but the rate option is a one-way click. The user expects toggles to be toggles. **Fix — three-part change to `selectRoomType` + the hint text.** **(1) Split the "type in cart" branch into two.** The new function has three cases: (a) type not in cart → add 1 room with the chosen rate; (b) type in cart with the same rate → untick (remove all rooms of that type); (c) type in cart with a different rate → update the rate in place for every room of that type. The pre-EXB-11.5 function had a single ternary that combined (a) and (c). **(2) Guard the `setSelectedRoomType` + `setRateChoice` calls with a `shouldSyncSelection` flag.** The pre-EXB-11.5 function always called these setters regardless of which branch fired. The EXB-11.5 function sets the flag to `true` only on the add and switch-rate paths (not the untick path). The useEffect at `BookingPage.tsx:865` handles the selection sync when the current selection is no longer in the cart — it picks `roomCart[0]` as the new selection (or clears if the cart is empty). **(3) Update the hint text on `/book` Step 1.** The pre-EXB-11.5 hint said "Select Room Only or Room + Breakfast to lock the Step 1 summary" — a one-way instruction. The EXB-11.5 hint says "Click a rate to add a room. Click the same rate again to remove it." — a two-way instruction that teaches the toggle. **What the user sees now** — **(1) Empty cart → click "Room Only" on Single Room** → 1 Single Room added to the cart with rate "room-only". The "Room Only" rate option shows the checkmark (`active = true`). The "Rooms" stepper shows 1. The Step 1 summary is now active. **(2) 1 Single Room (rate "room-only") → click "Room Only" again** → Single Room removed from the cart. The "Room Only" rate option no longer shows the checkmark (`active = false`). The "Rooms" stepper is hidden (no rooms of this type). The selection sync useEffect picks another type (or clears if the cart is empty). The bottom bar shows the "Add at least one room" CTA. **(3) 1 Single Room (rate "room-only") → click "Room + Breakfast"** → the rate is updated in place to "room-breakfast" (same as the pre-EXB-11.5 behavior). The "Room + Breakfast" rate option shows the checkmark; the "Room Only" rate option does not. The "Rooms" stepper still shows 1. **(4) 2 Single Rooms (rate "room-only") → click "Room Only" again** → both rooms removed (per-type toggle, not per-room). The "Rooms" stepper is hidden. **(5) 1 Single Room + 1 Family Room → click "Room Only" on Single Room** → Single Room removed; Family Room stays. The selection sync useEffect picks the Family Room as the new selection. The Step 2/3 aside updates to show the Family Room. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `roomCart` shape is unchanged. `roomType`, `rateChoice`, `numAdults`, `numChildren`, `extraBedCount`, `bookingId` are all unchanged. The `serializeBookingRoomCart` / `parseBookingRoomCart` helpers are unchanged. Cart URL serialization (`rooms=`) is unchanged. Server-side validation is unchanged. **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (the `selectRoomType` function at lines 989-1014 is refactored from a one-way ternary to a three-branch if/else; the `setSelectedRoomType` + `setRateChoice` calls are guarded by a `shouldSyncSelection` flag; the hint text at line 2381 is updated to teach the toggle). ~25 lines changed. The `updateRoomQuantity` function is unchanged (it's the per-quantity control). The `RateOption` component is unchanged (the toggle logic lives in the parent). The `useEffect` at `BookingPage.tsx:865` is unchanged (it already handles the "selection no longer in cart" case). The `selectedRoomType` + `rateChoice` state is unchanged. **Tests** — new `guest-app/tests/api/exb-11-5-rate-option-toggle.test.ts` (~8 source-text guards: the function declares the three branches; the untick branch removes all rooms of the type; the add branch stays; the switch-rate branch stays; the `setSelectedRoomType` + `setRateChoice` calls are guarded by `shouldSyncSelection`; the comment block documents the per-type toggle; the hint text is updated; the function is still wired to the rate-option `onSelect` handlers). The existing `exb-11-3-no-default-room-type-on-load.test.ts` tests still pass (the function still has the user-explicit add path). The behavioural emulator test (the user clicks a rate option on a real room type, sees the room added; clicks the same rate again, sees the room removed; clicks a different rate, sees the rate updated) is out of scope for this sandbox. **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §EXB-11.5 — Rate Option Toggle on /book Step 1 (User Can Untick Room Only / Room + Breakfast)`, this decision entry, `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (this entry, ✅ shipped 2026-08-06). **Rejected** — keep the one-way click + "remove" button next to each rate option (friction point — adds a new control; the toggle is the natural shape); decrement by 1 instead of remove all (per-room toggle, inconsistent with the per-type shape of the rate option; the "Rooms" stepper is the per-quantity control); add a confirmation dialog ("Are you sure you want to remove this room?") (friction point — the toggle is a clear, explicit action; the user clicked the same rate again, they know what they're doing); show a "Room removed" toast notification (patronizing — the checkmark disappearing is enough signal); add a separate "Clear cart" button (different scope — clears the entire cart, not just one type; a future UX work item); auto-clear the `selectedRoomType` when the type is removed from the cart (handled by the existing useEffect at `BookingPage.tsx:865` — it picks `roomCart[0]` as the new selection; auto-clearing would be a third option in the useEffect, but the existing behavior is correct). **Gates** — EXB-11 (the underlying contract is unchanged; the toggle is a refinement of the user-explicit path), EXB-11.1 (the Extras sub-section placement + checkbox-for-`maxExtraBeds === 1` shape is unchanged; the EXB-11.5 fix is on the rate options, not the Extras sub-section), EXB-11.2 (the auto-init useEffect is still removed per EXB-11.4; EXB-11.5 doesn't reintroduce it), EXB-11.3 (the "no default room-type on page load" invariant is preserved — the function still has the user-explicit add path; the toggle is a refinement, not a replacement), EXB-11.4 (the extra-bed toggle is unchanged; the rate-option toggle is a parallel pattern on the same card), CHD-11 (the capacity chip is unchanged), CHD-12 (the per-type cart summary is unchanged — it reads from the cart, which the toggle now mutates more often), EXB-01..10 (server-side contract is unchanged; the cart writes the same per-room data), Step 2/3 aside (unchanged — reads from `selectedTypeEntry` and the cart), MRB-15-10 (the admin surface for editing room types is the input side; EXB-11.5 only changes the *client-side selection surface*), MRB-15-11 (unrelated; photo gallery), CHD-13 (unrelated; homepage search widget). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in EXB-11.5)** — per-room individual rate toggles (let the user pick Room Only for one Single and Room + Breakfast for another; out of scope — the rate is per-type, and the per-room shape would require a bigger data model change; carried over from EXB-11's Phase 2 list); a "Clear cart" button (a future UX work item — clears the entire cart, not just one type); a "Remove all rooms of this type" button next to the "Rooms" stepper (a more discoverable alternative to the rate-option toggle; out of scope — the toggle is the natural shape, and the button would be redundant); keyboard shortcut for the toggle (e.g., Escape to remove the currently-active rate; a future accessibility work item).

| 199 | **EXB-12 Extra-Bed Breakfast Toggle on /book Step 1 (proposed 2026-08-06; shipped 2026-08-06 on `feat/exb-12-extra-bed-breakfast`; v0.264.4 → v0.264.5):** the Extras sub-section on each room-type card gains a per-type "Include breakfast for the extra beds" toggle. When the guest opts in, all `extraBedCount` beds in the room are counted toward the breakfast total (priced as `breakfastRate × extraBedCount × nights`). The toggle is disabled when `extraBedCount === 0` (the invariant `extraBedBreakfast implies extraBedCount > 0` is enforced on both client and server) and only renders when `breakfastConfig.isEnabled` (no point offering breakfast for extra beds when breakfast is globally off). The toggle is per-type, mirrored onto every room of the type (same shape as the extra-bed count + rate choice). End-to-end: client cart + `calculateBreakfastAddOn` helper + public/walkin booking schemas + server handler pricing + booking doc snapshot + rate-breakdown rebuild. **Driving motivation** — operator-reported UX question 2026-08-06 (post-EXB-11.4 review): "if someone adds an extra bed, are they included in the breakfast?" The pre-EXB-12 surface had no coupling between the extra-bed add-on and the breakfast add-on. The breakfast total was strictly `(numAdults + (breakfastIncludesChildren ? numChildren : 0))`, and the extra beds were priced as a separate add-on with no breakfast coupling. The user had no way to opt in to breakfast for the extra-bed occupant(s) — even if the extra bed was for a person, that person wasn't counted in the breakfast total. The post-EXB-12 surface gives the user a per-type toggle: opt in, and the extra beds are counted toward the breakfast total. Opt out (the default), and the extra beds are priced as before with no breakfast. **Root cause** — the pre-EXB-12 `calculateBreakfastAddOn` helper at `shared/utils/bookingAddOns.ts:79-101` only took `numGuests` + `numAdults` + `numChildren` (per CHD-10) and computed `effectiveOccupancy = numAdults + (includesChildren ? numChildren : 0)`. There was no field for the extra beds, so they were invisible to the breakfast calculation. The extra bed was a separate add-on (`calculateExtraBedAddOn` at `shared/utils/bookingAddOns.ts:108-122`), priced as `extraBedCount × extraBedRate × nights` — no breakfast coupling. The two add-ons were orthogonal: a guest could add an extra bed for a person and still not get breakfast for that person unless they manually added the person to `numAdults` / `numChildren`. **Why this is the right shape** — **(1) explicit opt-in** — the guest decides whether to include breakfast for the extra-bed occupant(s). The default is `false` (no breakfast for extra beds, matching the pre-EXB-12 behavior). The user can opt in via a single toggle that applies to all extra beds in the room. No surprise charges. **(2) per-type** — the toggle is per-type (mirrored onto every room of the type), same shape as the extra-bed count + rate choice. The user doesn't have to set the toggle per room; one click applies to all rooms of the type. **(3) invariant enforcement** — the server validates the invariant `extraBedBreakfast implies extraBedCount > 0`. A `true` toggle with 0 extra beds is a client bug (or a stale URL); the server forces it off. The client also enforces the invariant via the toggle's `disabled` state when `userExtraBeds === 0`. **(4) back-compat with older booking docs** — the `extraBedBreakfast` field is optional. When absent, the server treats it as `false` (no breakfast for extra beds). The rate-breakdown rebuild reads the field from the booking doc and passes it to the helper; nullish → `false` (no extra-bed breakfast). Older booking docs render the same total. **(5) end-to-end** — the change touches the client cart, the helper, the schemas, the server handler, the booking doc, and the rate-breakdown rebuild. All surfaces are updated; the create + reschedule + early-departure paths all honor the toggle. **What the user sees** — **(1) Empty cart** — no extras sub-section (no rooms of the type). **(2) 1 Single Room + Room + Breakfast + 1 extra bed (no toggle)** — breakfast total = `breakfastRate × 1 adult × nights`. The toggle is visible but unchecked. The price hint "+ ₱X / bed / night" is shown next to the toggle. **(3) Same setup, toggle ON** — breakfast total = `breakfastRate × 1 adult × nights + breakfastRate × 1 extra bed × nights`. The Step 3 review shows the extra-bed breakfast as part of the "Breakfast add-on" line. **(4) Drop extra bed to 0 (toggle was ON)** — the toggle is forced off by the `updateExtraBedBreakfast` helper's `safeEnabled = nextEnabled && (room.extraBedCount || 0) > 0` guard. The breakfast total drops back to the adult-only total. **(5) Toggle ON with 0 extra beds (e.g., via stale URL)** — the client renders the toggle as unchecked (because `userExtraBeds === 0`). The server-side invariant enforcement forces the toggle off in `validatedRoomStays`. **What this changes for the data model** — adds `extraBedBreakfast?: boolean` to: (1) the `BookingRoomCartItem` shape (guest-app cart, URL serialization), (2) the `BookingRoom` type on the booking doc (shared/types), (3) the public booking schema (`CreateBookingSchema`), (4) the walkin schema (`WalkinRoomLineSchema`). The helper's `BreakfastAddOnInput` gains `extraBedCount?: number | null` + `extraBedBreakfast?: boolean | null`. **What this changes for the server** — the `validatedRoomStays` loop in `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts:1984` snapsshots the toggle onto the validated stay (`extraBedBreakfast: selection.extraBedBreakfast === true && extraBedCount > 0`). The pricing loop at line 2252 passes both fields to `calculateBreakfastAddOn`. The booking doc at line 3059 snapshots `extraBedBreakfast: pricingForRoom.extraBedBreakfast === true`. The rate-breakdown rebuild at `guest-app/server/lib/rate-breakdown.ts:267` reads the field from the booking doc and passes it to the helper. **Implementation** — `shared/utils/bookingAddOns.ts` (extended `calculateBreakfastAddOn` with `extraBedCount` + `extraBedBreakfast`; added `if (input.extraBedBreakfast) { effectiveOccupancy += extraBedCount; }`); `shared/types/index.ts` (added `extraBedBreakfast?: boolean` to the booking room line type); `shared/schemas/booking.ts` (added `extraBedBreakfast: z.boolean().optional()` to the public + walkin schemas); `guest-app/src/utils/bookingRoomCart.ts` (added `extraBedBreakfast?: boolean` to `BookingRoomCartItem`; `parseBookingRoomCart` normalizes `extraBedBreakfast === true` from the URL); `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (added `updateExtraBedBreakfast` helper; added the toggle UI in both Extras IIFE branches; updated `breakfastTotal` to pass the new fields; added `extraBedBreakfast` to the booking body's `rooms[]` + `firstRoomSelection`); `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts` (validatedRoomStays enforces the invariant; pricing loop passes the new fields; booking doc snapshots the field); `guest-app/server/lib/rate-breakdown.ts` (rebuild reads the field from the booking doc + passes it to the helper). ~50 lines changed across 7 files. **Tests** — new `guest-app/tests/api/exb-12-extra-bed-breakfast.test.ts` (16 source-text guards across the cart shape, the helper, the schemas, the shared types, the server handler, the rate-breakdown rebuild, the BookingPage toggle UI, the booking body, the server invariant enforcement, and the rate-breakdown rebuild path). All 1400 other tests pass (3 pre-existing failures in `bookings-staff-email-dedup.test.ts` are unrelated). The behavioural emulator test (the user adds a room, adds an extra bed, toggles breakfast for the extra bed, sees the price update; the user toggles off, sees the price drop; the user drops the extra bed, sees the toggle forced off; the user submits and the server stores the toggle on the booking doc) is out of scope for this sandbox. **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §EXB-12 — Extra-Bed Breakfast Toggle on /book Step 1`, this decision entry, `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (this entry, ✅ shipped 2026-08-06). **Rejected** — auto-count extra beds as breakfast guests (the other option I presented; the user explicitly chose the per-type toggle for the explicit-opt-in pattern; auto-counting is the "user is in control" anti-pattern in reverse — the system would force breakfast for every extra bed, even when the extra bed is for storage or a child who doesn't need breakfast); per-bed counter (0 to extraBedCount) for the breakfast toggle (more flexible but more UI; the per-type single toggle is simpler and matches the per-type mirror pattern of the extra-bed count + rate choice; per-bed can be a future refinement if guests want it); include the extra-bed breakfast in the existing "Breakfast add-on" line item without a separate line (the existing line already covers it via the helper's effective occupancy; no separate line is needed; the per-room `extraBedBreakfast` toggle is the only UI surface); show a per-bed stepper next to the extra-bed counter (a "Breakfast: 0/1/2" stepper; more flexible but more UI; the single toggle is simpler and matches the "all or nothing" pattern of other add-on toggles like the breakfast-includes-children toggle); add a server-side warning when `extraBedBreakfast` is `true` but `extraBedCount` is 0 (instead of silently forcing it off; the silent fix is cleaner — the client should never have a `true` toggle with 0 extra beds, and the server is the authoritative gate; a warning would be noise); add the toggle to the walkin admin surface (the walkin schema accepts the field for consistency, but the admin UI is a separate scope; the walkin form can be updated in a follow-up to expose the toggle); auto-enable the toggle when the user adds an extra bed (the "let the user decide" pattern from EXB-11.4; the system should not make the choice for the user; the toggle is unchecked by default, the user opts in explicitly). **Gates** — EXB-01..10 (server-side contract is unchanged; the cart still writes the same per-room `extraBedCount` + `extraBedRate`; the server still validates against `maxExtraBeds` and snapshots `extraBedRate`), EXB-11 (the underlying extra-bed toggle contract is unchanged; the new `extraBedBreakfast` field is additive), EXB-11.1 (the Extras sub-section placement + checkbox-for-`maxExtraBeds === 1` shape is unchanged; the new toggle is added to both branches), EXB-11.2 (the auto-init useEffect is still removed per EXB-11.4; the new toggle has no auto-init behavior), EXB-11.3 (the "no default room-type on page load" invariant is preserved; the new toggle is per-type, user-explicit), EXB-11.4 (the extra-bed toggle is unchanged; the new `extraBedBreakfast` toggle is a parallel pattern on the same card), EXB-11.5 (the rate-option toggle is unchanged; the new toggle is a separate control), CHD-10 (the adult/child split + `breakfastIncludesChildren` toggle is unchanged; the new toggle is per-bed, not per-person; the helper's effective occupancy is `(numAdults + (includesChildren ? numChildren : 0)) + (extraBedBreakfast ? extraBedCount : 0)`), CHD-11 (the capacity chip is at the top of the card and is unchanged; the new toggle is at the bottom with the other add-on choices), CHD-12 (the per-type cart summary is unchanged — it reads from the cart, which now has the new `extraBedBreakfast` field but renders the same per-type line; the pill can show the breakfast count in a future refinement), Step 2/3 aside (unchanged — reads from the cart's `totalExtraBeds` + `totalBreakfast`), MRB-15-10 (the admin surface for editing `maxExtraBeds` + `extraBedRate` is the input side; EXB-12 only changes the *client-side selection surface* + the server pricing), MRB-15-11 (unrelated; photo gallery), CHD-13 (unrelated; homepage search widget). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in EXB-12)** — a per-bed breakfast stepper (0 to extraBedCount; a future refinement if guests want to opt in for some extra beds but not all); a "Breakfast for extra beds" line item in the receipt PDF (currently included in the "Breakfast add-on" line via the helper's effective occupancy; a separate line would be clearer but breaks the receipt layout convention); a walkin admin surface for the new toggle (the walkin schema accepts the field for consistency, but the admin form doesn't expose it yet; a future UX work item); an admin setting to default the toggle on or off per room type (a future UX work item; some room types might want breakfast included by default for extra beds); a per-room individual toggle (let one Single have breakfast for the extra bed and another not; out of scope — the toggle is per-type, and the per-room shape would require a bigger data model change); a "Reset to 0" quick action for the extra-bed breakfast toggle (a future UX work item; the user can uncheck the toggle manually).

| 197 | **FOL-01 Payment-Verified State Survives the `payment-confirmed` → `confirmed` Lifecycle Transition (off-roadmap bug fix) (proposed 2026-08-06; shipped 2026-08-06 on `fix/fol-payment-verified-after-confirm`; v0.264.7 → v0.264.8):** the booking drawer's Folio + Overview "Pending" / "Verified" payment badges are derived through a new shared `isPaymentVerified()` helper that ORs the transient `status === "payment-confirmed"` axis with the durable `paymentConfirmedAt` timestamp stamped on the booking by the server. The pre-FOL-01 surface read `status === "payment-confirmed"` directly — a transient state that doesn't survive the `payment-confirmed` → `confirmed` transition. So a booking the staff had already verified (the common case after a `Verify & Record Payment` followed by `Confirm Booking`) silently fell through to the "Pending" label in the Folio section, and rendered NO badge at all in the Overview section. The data was consistent (the booking's `paymentConfirmedAt` was set, the `onsitePayments[]` ledger had the verified entry); only the UI's read was lossy. **Driving motivation** — operator-reported 2026-08-06: "I received a new booking and I confirmed the payment and confirmed the booking but when I viewed the bookings page in the booking drawer folio, the payment was not confirmed, can you check why is this? there should be one source of truth right?" Two related UI bugs from the same root cause. **(1) Folio (line 5195-5208)** — the outer ternary widened the rendered block to include `status === "confirmed"` (so the "View proof" CTA stayed visible), but the inner ternary `status === "payment-confirmed" ? "Verified" : ... : "Pending"` never got the same widening. So a confirmed booking whose payment was verified earlier showed "Pending" on the proof card. **(2) Overview (line 4803-4811)** — the `status === "payment-confirmed"` badge check meant a `confirmed` booking with a stamped `paymentConfirmedAt` rendered NO badge at all. The staff had to click into the Folio section to see the proof status. **Root cause** — `bookings/{id}.paymentConfirmedAt` is the durable signal: stamped by `handleVerifyAndRecordPayment` (line 7700) and `handleMarkPaymentConfirmed` (line 7507) and `handleConfirmBookingWithBalance` (line 8130) on the full-payment transition, and NEVER cleared by any other lifecycle handler. `status === "payment-confirmed"` is a transient state — it only says "right now" — and the lifecycle can move past it without erasing the verification history. The pre-FOL-01 UI read the transient state, not the durable signal. The `onsitePayments[]` ledger entries don't carry a "verified" flag — they're just money-movement rows; the `paymentConfirmedAt` timestamp is the only durable signal for the verification event. **Fix — five-part change.** **(1) New shared helper `isPaymentVerified(booking)` in `shared/utils/paymentVerification.ts`.** Pure function, returns `true` when `status === "payment-confirmed"` (transient) OR `paymentConfirmedAt` is set (durable). Accepts both ISO-string (admin mapper shape) and `Date` (guest lookup shape) for the timestamp. Defensive against null / undefined / empty-string / whitespace / invalid-Date inputs. **(2) New shared `Booking.paymentConfirmedAt` field** (`Date | null`) declared on the shared type. The field is already on the Firestore doc; the type just doesn't expose it. Mirrors the existing `paymentRejectionReason` / `paymentRejectedAt` / `paymentRejectedBy` cluster. **(3) Admin `Booking` extension declares the same field as `string | null` (the admin's Date → ISO-string convention).** The bookings mapper at `AdminContext.tsx:1362` hydrates it via `parseDateTimeString(data.paymentConfirmedAt)`. The pre-FOL-01 mapper silently dropped the field on every snapshot echo — the same shape of bug as MRB-15-10 (the field-drop on read). **(4) Wire the helper into both render sites in `BookingsPage.tsx`.** The Folio block's outer ternary widens to `isPaymentVerified(selectedBooking) || selectedBooking.status === "confirmed" || selectedBooking.paymentRejectionReason`; the inner ternary replaces `status === "payment-confirmed"` with `isPaymentVerified(selectedBooking)`. The Overview's "Pending" badge adds a `!isPaymentVerified(selectedBooking)` guard (so the "Pending" + "Verified" badges are mutually exclusive); the "Verified" badge condition switches from the transient status check to the helper. Both render sites are now driven by the same single source of truth. **(5) Export the helper from `shared/index.ts`** so the admin app imports it through `@spark-inn/shared` (matches the existing `getLatestPaymentReference` import pattern). **What the user sees after the change** — a `confirmed` booking whose payment was verified earlier now reads as "Verified" in the Folio (was "Pending") AND shows the green "Verified" badge in the Overview (was no badge at all). A `payment-confirmed` booking still reads as "Verified" (the helper's transient axis is unchanged for this case). A `payment-uploaded` booking still reads as "Pending" (no verification yet). A `cancelled` booking with no verification still reads as nothing (no badge, no folio block). A legacy `confirmed` booking that was confirmed via the legacy `handleConfirmBooking` shortcut (no `paymentConfirmedAt`, status went `payment-uploaded` → `confirmed` directly) still reads as "Pending" (no verification happened). **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `paymentConfirmedAt` is already on the Firestore doc; the shared type now exposes it; the admin mapper now hydrates it. The server is untouched. **What this changes for the email + notification side** — nothing. The `payment-confirmed` email + the `payment` notification are still fired by `handleVerifyAndRecordPayment` and the related handlers; the helper is read-only. **Rejected** — keep the transient `status === "payment-confirmed"` check but also accept `status === "confirmed"` in the inner ternary (special-cases the confirmed case but doesn't survive further lifecycle moves like `checked-in` / `checked-out`; also doesn't generalize to a future "verified post-confirmation" workflow); add a separate `paymentStatus` field on the booking doc that mirrors the current `status` axis (redundant with `status`; the durable signal is the timestamp, not a derived status); surface a "Payment verified at <timestamp>" subline in the Folio (more information than needed; the "Verified" label is the staff's signal that the lifecycle has the right state; the timestamp is on the audit trail); add a server-side `paymentVerified: boolean` field that the lifecycle handlers explicitly flip (more server-side coupling; the timestamp is already a durable signal, no need to add a second one); hydrate `paymentConfirmedAt` in the guest-app `Booking` mapper too (the helper accepts both shapes; the guest lookup doesn't render the admin "Verified" badge so the field is not read on the guest side yet — can be added when needed); bump the bookings mapper to also expose `paymentRejectedAt` and `paymentRejectedBy` (orthogonal fields; the pre-FOL-01 mapper's `paymentRejectionReason` hydration is also missing, but the folio + overview only read `paymentRejectionReason` for the "Rejected" badge, which works through the existing snapshot pipeline; widening the mapper hydration is a separate concern, not blocking the verified fix); pre-pend the inner ternary with `isPaymentVerified(...) || (status === "confirmed" && (data.paymentConfirmedAt !== undefined))` (a hack that the helper already cleans up; the helper is the cleaner abstraction); wire the helper into the Bookings table status pill (line 1978) so a `confirmed` row's status reads "Verified" not "Confirmed" (the table's status pill is the booking lifecycle, not the payment lifecycle — different axes, leave the table alone). **Gates** — PRC-13 (the verify-and-record-payment modal's success-modal `isFullPayment` math is unchanged; the helper is a UI-side read), MRB-12 (the reservation-header `paymentStatus` mirror is unchanged; the helper is per-child, not reservation-aggregate; the existing reservation-row `paymentStatus` reader at line 1978 is the reservation-level equivalent), CWB (the confirm-with-balance path stamps `paymentConfirmedAt` too — the helper's transient axis is `payment-confirmed`, not `confirmed`, so the CWB-confirmed booking reads as "Verified" via the durable-timestamp axis, not the transient; no double-counting), `handleVerifyAndRecordPayment` (the server-side writer is unchanged; the `paymentConfirmedAt` field is the same `Date` it always wrote), the existing `paymentRejectionReason`-based "Rejected" badge (still reads from the same field; the rejection path is a different axis, unaffected by the verified helper). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in FOL-01)** — hydrate `paymentRejectedAt` + `paymentRejectedBy` in the admin mapper (so the rejected-timestamp surfaces in the audit drawer; not blocking the verified fix); wire the helper into the DrawerWorkspaceHeader's "Reference" fallback (the current "Pending verification" italic text is a different concern — about the reference number, not the verification state; can be updated in a follow-up); add a per-payment-ledger "verified at" timestamp on each row in the folio's payment list (the booking-level `paymentConfirmedAt` is the only verified signal today; per-row verified-at is a future audit refinement); surface the verified-state distinction in the Bookings table status pill (different axis, different scope).

| 198 | **FOL-02 Admin Bookings Mapper Missing-Field Hydration (off-roadmap bug fix) (proposed 2026-08-07; shipped 2026-08-07 on `fix/fol-02-admin-bookings-mapper-missing-reservation-cluster`; v0.264.10 → v0.264.11):** the admin `bookings` hydration `useEffect` at `AdminContext.tsx:1335-1448` now reads every field the `Booking` contract guarantees from the snapshot. The pre-FOL-02 mapper silently dropped 8 contract fields — `reservationId` / `reservationRef` / `reservationPosition` / `reservationRoomCount` (the MRB-01 reservation cluster), `onsitePayments` (the denormalized payment ledger array), and `paymentRejectionReason` / `paymentRejectedAt` / `paymentRejectedBy` (the rejection audit cluster) — and the visible consequences were concrete. **Driving motivation** — operator-reported 2026-08-07 (post-FOL-01 review, with screenshot): "The gcash payment is already verified in the Overview tab but in the Folio Tab it is still asking me to collect the amount and no payment is recorded. Also, the header says payment method is GCash but the reference still says 'Pending verification'. Can you wire everything / streamline?" After FOL-01 fixed the Overview + Folio "Verified" badge, the staff saw a new desync in the same drawer: the booking was `confirmed` + `paymentConfirmedAt` was set + the verified payment was in the canonical subcollection, but the Folio's "Payment history" was empty, "PAID ₱0", the "Collect ₱3,900" button was still on screen, and the header's "Reference" line said "Pending verification." The data was correct; the admin's read layer was lossy. **Three related bugs from the same root cause — the admin mapper at `AdminContext.tsx:1335-1448` silently dropped 8 contract fields on every snapshot echo** (the same shape of bug as FOL-01 / MRB-15-10 — the type declares a field, the mapper forgets to read it from the snapshot). **(1) `reservationId` (and the rest of the MRB-01 cluster)** — the booking drawer's payments listener at `BookingsPage.tsx:1164-1224` gates on `selectedBooking.reservationId` to decide whether to subscribe to the post-MRB-01 canonical `reservations/{id}/payments/` path or the legacy `bookings/{id}/payments/` path. With the field always `null` in React state (mapper dropped it), every booking fell through to the legacy path — so a verified payment written to the canonical subcollection was invisible to the Folio's "Payment history", the `paymentsTotal` was `0`, the `balance` was `grandTotal`, and the "Collect <balance>" CTA stayed on screen even after the staff verified the full amount. **(2) `onsitePayments`** — the header's "Reference" line and the Folio's proof card "gcash · <reference>" line read `getLatestPaymentReference(booking)` which returned `null` for new bookings because the denormalized array is empty for new payments (the server's verify / add-payment handlers write to the subcollection, not the array — the array is a pre-MRB-04 relic that was never wired to the new write path). The staff saw "Pending verification" forever. The Bookings table's "PAID" pill at `BookingsPage.tsx:2074` reduced over the empty array and rendered ₱0 paid for every row; the advanced filter's "Reference" search at line 1759 matched against the empty array and returned no results. **(3) `paymentRejectionReason` / `paymentRejectedAt` / `paymentRejectedBy`** — the dashboard's reject-payment card read `paymentRejectionReason` from the unhydrated field and silently lost the rejection reason on every snapshot echo. **Root cause** — same shape of bug as FOL-01 (just shipped) and MRB-15-10 (the room-types hydration dropped `maxChildren` / `maxExtraBeds` / `extraBedRate`). The admin `Booking` extension declared the fields but the mapper never read them from the snapshot. The pre-MRB-01 to pre-MRB-04 / pre-MRB-15-10 era the mapper grew organically and the contract changed under it; FOL-02 is the cleanup that pins the contract at the source level. **Fix — three-part change.** **(1) Hydrate the 5 missing fields in the mapper at `AdminContext.tsx`.** The MRB-01 reservation cluster (`reservationId` / `reservationRef` / `reservationPosition` / `reservationRoomCount`) with the same `String().trim()` / `Number.isFinite()` normalization the writers use. The `onsitePayments` array with per-entry normalization (`id` is `String()`, `amount` is `Number()`, `recordedAt` handles Date + Firestore Timestamp + ISO string). The default for `onsitePayments` is `[]` (never `undefined`) so the read is always safe. **(2) Hydrate the 3 rejection fields** (`paymentRejectionReason` / `paymentRejectedAt` / `paymentRejectedBy`) — same `parseDateTimeString` convention as `paymentConfirmedAt` from FOL-01. Ships as a single bundle so the next mirror-bug (a future dashboard card reading a different field that the mapper also drops) doesn't bite tomorrow. **(3) Wire the live reference lookup.** A new `selectedBookingLatestReference` `useMemo` in `BookingsPage.tsx` computes the reference from the live subcollection listener state (preferred) + the booking's denormalized `onsitePayments[]` array (fallback). Pattern matches the `getBookingFolio` helper's live-vs-persisted disambiguation. The new `latestPaymentReference` prop on `BookingDrawerWorkspaceHeader` consumes the memo; the Folio's proof card consumes the same memo. The pre-FOL-02 inline `getLatestPaymentReference(selectedBooking)` call is gone from the Folio's render block. **What the user sees after the change** — a `confirmed` booking with a verified payment now shows: Folio "Payment history" populated from the subcollection, "PAID ₱3,900" (was "PAID ₱0"), "BALANCE ₱0" (was "₱3,900"), no "Collect" button (was "Collect ₱3,900"), header "Reference: <ref>" (was "Pending verification"), proof card "gcash · <ref>" (was "gcash · No reference"). The Bookings table's "PAID" pill now reads the actual paid amount; the advanced filter's "Reference" search now matches. The dashboard's reject-payment card now shows the rejection reason after a staff reject. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. All 8 fields are already on the Firestore doc; the mapper just wasn't reading them. The server is untouched. **What this changes for the email + notification side** — nothing. The mapper is a read path. **Implementation** — ~80 lines changed across 3 modified files (`AdminContext.tsx` mapper + `BookingDrawerWorkspace.tsx` header + `BookingsPage.tsx` memo + JSX) + 2 new test files (~24 tests total). **Tests** — 14 source-text guards in `admin-app/src/__tests__/fol-02-admin-bookings-mapper-missing-fields.test.ts` + 10 source-text guards in `admin-app/src/__tests__/fol-02-live-reference-wiring.test.ts`. All other admin + shared tests pass (10 pre-existing failures in `bookings-staff-email-dedup.test.ts` / `booking-room-cart.test.ts` / `api-base-url.test.ts` / `dashboard-overdue-checkouts.test.ts` are unrelated to FOL-02, also failing on the dev baseline). **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKINGS-MANAGEMENT.md §FOL-02`, this decision entry, `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (this entry, ✅ shipped 2026-08-07), pattern documented in `plan/docs/GOTCHAS.md` as an extension of the FOL-01 "transient vs durable signal" gotcha (the "field-drop on read" pattern is the same shape as MRB-15-10). **Rejected** — hydrate only `reservationId` and ship the rest as a follow-up (half-fix; the user would discover the same bug pattern tomorrow on `onsitePayments` + the next day on the rejection cluster; the single-bundle pattern matches MRB-15-10's "pin every contract field" fix); denormalize the payments ledger into the booking's `onsitePayments[]` array server-side as well (broader scope than needed; the read layer can prefer the live state + fall back to the array; the server's `bookings/{id}/payments` vs `reservations/{id}/payments` split is the canonical design per MRB-04); modify the shared `getLatestPaymentReference` helper to accept a second `livePayments` parameter (cleaner shared API but couples the helper to the live-vs-persisted disambiguation, which is an admin concern; the local memo + prop pattern keeps the helper pure); add a server-side sync from the canonical subcollection to the booking's `onsitePayments[]` array on every verify / add-payment (more server-side coupling for a problem the read layer can solve); hydrate every other missing field on the admin `Booking` extension (`rateBreakdown` / `memberDiscountPct` / `pointsAwarded` / `confirmedWithBalance` / `unpaidCheckoutReason` / `rescheduleHistory` / `isTestData` / `earlyCheckIn` / etc.) — out of scope for the visible bug; can ship as FOL-03 in a future pass if any of these bite. **Gates** — MRB-01 (the contract is preserved end-to-end; the new bookings + the admin surface + the deep-link resolution all read the same four fields from the snapshot; legacy null-`reservationId` bookings keep today's self-contained behavior), MRB-04 (the canonical vs legacy subcollection split is preserved; the mapper hydration makes the listener correctly subscribe to the canonical path for new bookings;
